```js
// The editor reads and changes the site links here.
app.get("/api/admin/links", adminLimiter, adminAuth, async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    res.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    res.json({ fields: links.FIELDS, values: await store.readLinks(0) });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Admin links read failed:", err.message);
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not read the sheet. Please try again shortly." });
  }
});

app.put("/api/admin/links", adminLimiter, adminAuth, async (req, res) => {
  const { values, errors } = links.sanitize(req.body);
  if (Object.keys(errors).length) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors });
  try {
    await store.saveLinks(values);
    res.json({ ok: true, values });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Admin links save failed:", err.message);
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not save to the sheet. Please try again shortly." });
  }
});

// The editor reads and changes the chapter video addresses here.
app.get("/api/admin/videos", adminLimiter, adminAuth, async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    res.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    res.json({ chapters: videos.VIDEO_CHAPTERS, values: await store.readVideos(0) });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Admin videos read failed:", err.message);
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not read the sheet. Please try again shortly." });
  }
});

app.put("/api/admin/videos", adminLimiter, adminAuth, async (req, res) => {
  const { values, errors } = videos.sanitize(req.body);
  if (Object.keys(errors).length) return res.status(400).json({ error: "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors });
  try {
    await store.saveVideos(values);
    res.json({ ok: true, values });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Admin videos save failed:", err.message);
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not save to the sheet. Please try again shortly." });
  }
});

app.get("/api/admin/results", adminLimiter, adminAuth, async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    const quiz = await store.readQuiz();
    res.json({ results: quiz.map(quizEntry).reverse(), scale: grading.SCALE });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Admin results failed:", err.message);
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not read the sheet. Please try again shortly." });
  }
});

// ---- Database view: the editor browses, adds, edits and deletes records from /admin ----
// What may be changed, and the checks on every change, live in admin-database.js. Only a WRONG password counts
// toward the lock-out here (a mistyped value must not lock the editor out of editing); a second limiter just
// stops floods, because every call here reads from Google.
const database = adminDatabase.create(store);
app.use(
  "/api/admin/database",
  rateLimit({ windowMs: 60 * 1000, max: 120, standardHeaders: true, legacyHeaders: false, message: { error: "Too many requests. Please wait a moment." } }),
  rateLimit({
    windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000,
    max: 15,
    skipSuccessfulRequests: true,
    requestWasSuccessful: (_req, res) => res.statusCode !== 401,
    standardHeaders: true,
    legacyHeaders: false,
    message: { error: "Too many attempts. Try again in a few minutes." },
  }),
  adminAuth
);

function databaseRoute(handler) {
  return async (req, res) => {
    res.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    try {
      await handler(req, res);
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof adminDatabase.AdminError) {
        return res.status(err.status).json({ error: err.message, ...(err.fields ? { errors: err.fields } : {}) });
      }
      console.error("Admin database failed:", err.message);
      res.status(500).json({ error: "Could not reach the sheet. Please try again shortly." });
    }
  };
}
// Changes wait their turn with registrations (or handbook saves), so a row number never moves under a running save.
const inQueue = (key, task) => (key === "progress" ? exclusiveProgress : exclusive)(task);

app.get("/api/admin/database", databaseRoute(async (_req, res) => res.json(database.meta())));

app.get("/api/admin/database/:key", databaseRoute(async (req, res) => res.json(await database.read(req.params.key))));

app.put("/api/admin/database/:key/rows/:n", databaseRoute(async (req, res) => {
  const b = req.body || {};
  res.json(await inQueue(req.params.key, () => database.update(req.params.key, req.params.n, b.original, b.values)));
}));

app.post("/api/admin/database/:key/rows", databaseRoute(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await inQueue(req.params.key, () => database.create(req.params.key, req.body)));
}));

app.post("/api/admin/database/:key/rows/:n/delete", databaseRoute(async (req, res) => {
  res.json(await inQueue(req.params.key, () => database.remove(req.params.key, req.params.n, (req.body || {}).original)));
}));

app.listen(PORT, async () => {
  console.log(`ABECFX registration running on port ${PORT}`);
  const ka = require("./keepalive").startKeepAlive();
  if (ka) console.log("Keep-alive pings on:", ka.target);
  try {
    await store.init();
    console.log("Google Sheet ready.");
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Google Sheet setup problem:", err.message);
  }
});
```

#### `sheets.js`

```js
// Thin Google Sheets storage layer (service account + REST API).
const { JWT } = require("google-auth-library");
const links = require("./links");
const videos = require("./videos");

const SHEET_ID = process.env.GOOGLE_SHEET_ID;
const TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_SHEET_TAB || "Students";
const PHOTO_TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_PHOTO_TAB || "Photos";
const API = "https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets";

const STATUSES = [
  "Uncertified",
  "Certified",
  "Graduate",
  "Alumni Member",
  "Mentor",
  "Staff Member",
  "CEO",
];

const HEADERS = [
  "Reg Number",
  "Registered At (Lagos)",
  "Country",
  "Programme Type",
  "Full Name",
  "Email",
  "WhatsApp Number",
  "Gender",
  "Mentorship Code",
  "Status",
  "Status Date",
  "ID Issue Date",
  "State / Region",
  "Date of Birth",
];
const LAST_COL = "N";
const PHOTO_HEADERS = ["Reg Number", "Photo (base64 JPEG - do not edit)"];
const QUIZ_TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_QUIZ_TAB || "Quiz Results";
const QUIZ_HEADERS = [
  "Submitted At (Lagos)",
  "Reg Number",
  "Full Name",
  "Score",
  "Total Questions",
  "Percent",
  "Grade",
  "Attempt",
  "Telegram ID",
  "Breakdown (JSON)",
  "Submitted (UTC)",
];
const QUIZ_LAST_COL = "K";

// Mentorship codes: one row per code. The editor types new codes into column A; a code works once.
const CODE_TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_CODE_TAB || "Mentorship Codes";
const CODE_HEADERS = ["Mentorship Code", "Created At (Lagos)", "Used By (Reg Number)", "Used At (Lagos)"];
const CODE_LAST_COL = "D";

// Handbook progress: one row per student (reg number, name, chapters read, count, total, percent, updated).
const PROG_TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_PROGRESS_TAB || "Handbook Progress";
const PROG_HEADERS = ["Reg Number", "Full Name", "Chapters Read", "Chapters Read (count)", "Total Chapters", "Percent", "Updated At (Lagos)"];
const PROG_LAST_COL = "G";

// Site links (social icons, affiliate link, contact, About text): one row per setting, edited from /admin.
const LINKS_TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_LINKS_TAB || "Site Links";
const LINKS_HEADERS = ["Key (do not edit)", "What it is", "Value"];
const LINKS_LAST_COL = "C";

// Chapter videos: one row per handbook chapter 01-12 with its YouTube address, edited from /admin.
const VIDEO_TAB = process.env.GOOGLE_VIDEO_TAB || "Chapter Videos";
const VIDEO_HEADERS = ["Chapter", "Chapter title", "YouTube video address"];
const VIDEO_LAST_COL = "C";

function loadCredentials() {
  if (process.env.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON) {
    const c = JSON.parse(process.env.GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON);
    return { email: c.client_email, key: c.private_key };
  }
  return {
    email: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_EMAIL,
    key: (process.env.GOOGLE_PRIVATE_KEY || "").replace(/\\n/g, "\n"),
  };
}

let client;
function getClient() {
  if (client) return client;
  const { email, key } = loadCredentials();
  if (!SHEET_ID || !email || !key) {
    throw new Error(
      "Google Sheets is not configured. Set GOOGLE_SHEET_ID and GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON."
    );
  }
  client = new JWT({
    email,
    key,
    scopes: ["https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets"],
  });
  return client;
}

async function call(path, options = {}) {
  const res = await getClient().request({
    url: `${API}/${SHEET_ID}${path}`,
    ...options,
  });
  return res.data;
}

const range = (r, tab = TAB) => encodeURIComponent(`${tab}!${r}`);

async function findTab(title) {
  const meta = await call("?fields=sheets.properties(sheetId,title)");
  return (meta.sheets || []).map((s) => s.properties).find((p) => p.title === title);
}

async function ensureTab(title) {
  let tab = await findTab(title);
  if (!tab) {
    await call(":batchUpdate", {
      method: "POST",
      data: { requests: [{ addSheet: { properties: { title } } }] },
    });
    tab = await findTab(title);
  }
  return tab;
}

async function ensureHeaders(tab, headers, lastCol) {
  const head = await call(`/values/${range(`A1:${lastCol}1`, tab)}`);
  const current = (head.values && head.values[0]) || [];
  if (headers.some((h, i) => current[i] !== h)) {
    await call(`/values/${range(`A1:${lastCol}1`, tab)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
      method: "PUT",
      data: { values: [headers] },
    });
  }
}

// Make sure both tabs exist with headers, and the Status column is a dropdown.
async function init() {
  const students = await ensureTab(TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(TAB, HEADERS, LAST_COL);

  const photos = await ensureTab(PHOTO_TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(PHOTO_TAB, PHOTO_HEADERS, "B");

  await ensureTab(QUIZ_TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(QUIZ_TAB, QUIZ_HEADERS, QUIZ_LAST_COL);

  await ensureTab(CODE_TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(CODE_TAB, CODE_HEADERS, CODE_LAST_COL);

  await ensureTab(PROG_TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(PROG_TAB, PROG_HEADERS, PROG_LAST_COL);

  await ensureTab(LINKS_TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(LINKS_TAB, LINKS_HEADERS, LINKS_LAST_COL);
  // First run only: fill the tab with the starting links. After that the sheet / dashboard is the truth.
  const existing = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:A`, LINKS_TAB)}`);
  if (!(existing.values && existing.values.length)) {
    await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${LINKS_LAST_COL}${links.FIELDS.length + 1}`, LINKS_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
      method: "PUT",
      data: { values: links.toRows(links.DEFAULTS) },
    });
  }

  await ensureTab(VIDEO_TAB);
  await ensureHeaders(VIDEO_TAB, VIDEO_HEADERS, VIDEO_LAST_COL);
  const vExisting = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:A`, VIDEO_TAB)}`);
  if (!(vExisting.values && vExisting.values.length)) {
    await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${VIDEO_LAST_COL}${videos.VIDEO_CHAPTERS.length + 1}`, VIDEO_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
      method: "PUT",
      data: { values: videos.toRows({}) },
    });
  }

  await call(":batchUpdate", {
    method: "POST",
    data: {
      requests: [
        {
          // Dropdown on the Status column (column J) so editors pick from a fixed list.
          setDataValidation: {
            range: { sheetId: students.sheetId, startRowIndex: 1, startColumnIndex: 9, endColumnIndex: 10 },
            rule: {
              condition: {
                type: "ONE_OF_LIST",
                values: STATUSES.map((s) => ({ userEnteredValue: s })),
              },
              showCustomUi: true,
              strict: true,
            },
          },
        },
        {
          // Keep the long photo text from stretching rows.
          repeatCell: {
            range: { sheetId: photos.sheetId, startRowIndex: 1, startColumnIndex: 1, endColumnIndex: 2 },
            cell: { userEnteredFormat: { wrapStrategy: "CLIP" } },
            fields: "userEnteredFormat.wrapStrategy",
          },
        },
      ],
    },
  });
}

async function readAll() {
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${LAST_COL}`)}`);
  return data.values || [];
}

// Short cache for the public lookup page so heavy use can't exhaust Google's read quota.
let cache = { at: 0, rows: [] };
async function readCached(ttlMs = 15000) {
  if (Date.now() - cache.at > ttlMs) {
    cache = { rows: await readAll(), at: Date.now() };
  }
  return cache.rows;
}

async function appendRow(row) {
  await call(
    `/values/${range(`A:${LAST_COL}`)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
    { method: "POST", data: { values: [row] } }
  );
  cache.at = 0;
}

// ---- Quiz results (one row per finished quiz attempt, written by the Telegram quiz bot) ----
let quizCache = { at: 0, rows: [] };
async function readQuiz(ttlMs = 15000) {
  if (Date.now() - quizCache.at > ttlMs) {
    const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${QUIZ_LAST_COL}`, QUIZ_TAB)}`);
    quizCache = { rows: data.values || [], at: Date.now() };
  }
  return quizCache.rows;
}

async function appendQuiz(row) {
  await call(
    `/values/${range(`A:${QUIZ_LAST_COL}`, QUIZ_TAB)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
    { method: "POST", data: { values: [row] } }
  );
  quizCache.at = 0;
}

// ---- Handbook progress (one row per student; saved by the portal's "Mark as read" button) ----
let progCache = { at: 0, rows: [] };
async function readProgress(ttlMs = 5000) {
  if (Date.now() - progCache.at > ttlMs) {
    const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${PROG_LAST_COL}`, PROG_TAB)}`);
    progCache = { rows: data.values || [], at: Date.now() };
  }
  return progCache.rows;
}

// The cell holding the chapters a student has read, or "" if the student has no row yet.
async function getProgressCell(reg, ttlMs) {
  const rows = await readProgress(ttlMs);
  const r = rows.find((x) => (x[0] || "") === reg);
  return r ? r[2] || "" : "";
}

// Create or update the student's row. Callers serialise writes so two saves cannot create two rows.
async function saveProgress(reg, fullName, readIds, total, percent, updatedAt) {
  const rows = await readProgress(0);
  const i = rows.findIndex((x) => (x[0] || "") === reg);
  const row = [reg, fullName, readIds.join(","), readIds.length, total, percent, updatedAt];
  if (i >= 0) {
    await call(`/values/${range(`A${i + 2}:${PROG_LAST_COL}${i + 2}`, PROG_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
      method: "PUT",
      data: { values: [row] },
    });
  } else {
    await call(
      `/values/${range(`A:${PROG_LAST_COL}`, PROG_TAB)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
      { method: "POST", data: { values: [row] } }
    );
  }
  progCache.at = 0;
}

// ---- Mentorship codes (always read fresh: a code must never be accepted twice) ----
const normCode = (v) => String(v || "").trim().toUpperCase();

// Returns { row, sheetRow } for an unused code, or null if the code is unknown or already used.
async function findFreeCode(code) {
  const want = normCode(code);
  if (!want) return null;
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${CODE_LAST_COL}`, CODE_TAB)}`);
  const rows = data.values || [];
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    if (normCode(rows[i][0]) === want) {
      if ((rows[i][2] || "").trim()) return null; // already used
      return { code: want, sheetRow: i + 2 };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

async function markCodeUsed(sheetRow, reg, usedAt) {
  await call(`/values/${range(`C${sheetRow}:D${sheetRow}`, CODE_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
    method: "PUT",
    data: { values: [[reg, usedAt]] },
  });
}

// ---- Site links ----
let linksCache = { at: 0, values: null };
async function readLinks(ttlMs = 30000) {
  if (!linksCache.values || Date.now() - linksCache.at > ttlMs) {
    const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${LINKS_LAST_COL}`, LINKS_TAB)}`);
    linksCache = { values: links.fromRows(data.values || []), at: Date.now() };
  }
  return linksCache.values;
}

async function saveLinks(values) {
  await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${LINKS_LAST_COL}${links.FIELDS.length + 1}`, LINKS_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
    method: "PUT",
    data: { values: links.toRows(values) },
  });
  linksCache = { at: Date.now(), values };
}

// ---- Chapter videos ----
let videoCache = { at: 0, values: null };
async function readVideos(ttlMs = 30000) {
  if (!videoCache.values || Date.now() - videoCache.at > ttlMs) {
    const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${VIDEO_LAST_COL}`, VIDEO_TAB)}`);
    videoCache = { values: videos.fromRows(data.values || []), at: Date.now() };
  }
  return videoCache.values;
}

async function saveVideos(values) {
  await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${VIDEO_LAST_COL}${videos.VIDEO_CHAPTERS.length + 1}`, VIDEO_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, {
    method: "PUT",
    data: { values: videos.toRows(values) },
  });
  videoCache = { at: Date.now(), values };
}

// ---- Passport photos (kept on their own tab so student reads stay small and fast) ----
let photoIndex = { at: 0, regs: [] };
async function photoRegs(ttlMs) {
  if (Date.now() - photoIndex.at > ttlMs) {
    const data = await call(`/values/${range("A2:A", PHOTO_TAB)}`);
    photoIndex = { at: Date.now(), regs: (data.values || []).map((r) => r[0] || "") };
  }
  return photoIndex.regs;
}

const photoCache = new Map();

async function appendPhoto(reg, base64) {
  await call(
    `/values/${range("A:B", PHOTO_TAB)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
    { method: "POST", data: { values: [[reg, base64]] } }
  );
  photoIndex.at = 0;
  photoCache.delete(reg);
}

async function getPhoto(reg) {
  const hit = photoCache.get(reg);
  if (hit && Date.now() - hit.at < 10 * 60 * 1000) return hit.b64;

  let regs = await photoRegs(30000);
  let i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  if (i < 0) {
    regs = await photoRegs(5000); // maybe a brand-new registration
    i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  }
  if (i < 0) return null;

  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`B${i + 2}`, PHOTO_TAB)}`);
  const b64 = (data.values && data.values[0] && data.values[0][0]) || null;
  if (b64) {
    photoCache.set(reg, { b64, at: Date.now() });
    if (photoCache.size > 300) photoCache.delete(photoCache.keys().next().value);
  }
  return b64;
}

// ---- Generic tab access for the editor dashboard's "Sheets" view (the rules live in admin-sheets.js) ----
// The Photos tab is deliberately left out: it holds long image text that nobody should edit by hand.
const EDITOR_TABS = {
  students: { title: TAB, headers: HEADERS, lastCol: LAST_COL },
  codes: { title: CODE_TAB, headers: CODE_HEADERS, lastCol: CODE_LAST_COL },
  quiz: { title: QUIZ_TAB, headers: QUIZ_HEADERS, lastCol: QUIZ_LAST_COL },
  progress: { title: PROG_TAB, headers: PROG_HEADERS, lastCol: PROG_LAST_COL },
  links: { title: LINKS_TAB, headers: LINKS_HEADERS, lastCol: LINKS_LAST_COL },
  videos: { title: VIDEO_TAB, headers: VIDEO_HEADERS, lastCol: VIDEO_LAST_COL },
  // Internal only: never listed in the dashboard. Used to remove a deleted student's photo.
  photos: { title: PHOTO_TAB, headers: PHOTO_HEADERS, lastCol: "B" },
};

function editorTab(key) {
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(EDITOR_TABS, key)) throw new Error("Unknown sheet: " + key);
  return EDITOR_TABS[key];
}

const cellText = (v) => (v == null ? "" : String(v));
// Google trims empty cells at the end of a row; give every row its full width again.
function fitRow(row, width) {
  const r = (row || []).slice(0, width).map(cellText);
  while (r.length < width) r.push("");
  return r;
}
const colLetter = (i) => String.fromCharCode(65 + i);
const quoteTab = (title) => "'" + title.replace(/'/g, "''") + "'";

// Forget whatever the website has remembered about a tab, so the next read sees the change.
function invalidate(key) {
  if (key === "students") cache.at = 0;
  else if (key === "quiz") quizCache.at = 0;
  else if (key === "progress") progCache.at = 0;
  else if (key === "links") linksCache = { at: 0, values: null };
  else if (key === "videos") videoCache = { at: 0, values: null };
  else if (key === "photos") { photoIndex.at = 0; photoCache.clear(); }
}

function sheetUrl() {
  return SHEET_ID ? `https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/${encodeURIComponent(SHEET_ID)}/edit` : "";
}

// Every data row of a tab, always read fresh. `n` is the row number in the sheet (the header is row 1).
async function tabRows(key) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}`);
  return (data.values || []).map((r, i) => ({ n: i + 2, cells: fitRow(r, t.headers.length) }));
}

async function tabRow(key, n) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A${n}:${t.lastCol}${n}`, t.title)}`);
  return fitRow((data.values || [])[0], t.headers.length);
}

// Change only the cells that changed ({ columnIndex: text }), so the rest of the row is left exactly as it was.
// RAW means the text is stored as typed: a value starting with "=" can never run as a formula.
async function writeTabCells(key, n, changes) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const data = Object.keys(changes).map((i) => ({
    range: `${quoteTab(t.title)}!${colLetter(Number(i))}${n}`,
    values: [[cellText(changes[i])]],
  }));
  if (!data.length) return;
  await call("/values:batchUpdate", { method: "POST", data: { valueInputOption: "RAW", data } });
  invalidate(key);
}

async function appendTabRows(key, rows) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  if (!rows.length) return;
  await call(
    `/values/${range(`A:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
    { method: "POST", data: { values: rows.map((r) => fitRow(r, t.headers.length)) } }
  );
  invalidate(key);
}

async function deleteTabRow(key, n) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const tab = await findTab(t.title);
  if (!tab) throw new Error("The sheet tab was not found: " + t.title);
  await call(":batchUpdate", {
    method: "POST",
    data: {
      requests: [{ deleteDimension: { range: { sheetId: tab.sheetId, dimension: "ROWS", startIndex: n - 1, endIndex: n } } }],
    },
  });
  invalidate(key);
}

// Delete every row of a tab whose column A equals `reg` (a deleted student's photo, results, progress).
// Rows are removed from the bottom up in ONE request, so the row numbers cannot shift under it.
async function deleteTabRowsByReg(key, reg, col = 0) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const tab = await findTab(t.title);
  if (!tab) return 0;
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}`);
  const hits = [];
  (data.values || []).forEach((r, i) => { if (String((r && r[col]) || "").trim().toUpperCase() === reg) hits.push(i + 2); });
  if (!hits.length) return 0;
  await call(":batchUpdate", {
    method: "POST",
    data: {
      requests: hits.reverse().map((n) => ({
        deleteDimension: { range: { sheetId: tab.sheetId, dimension: "ROWS", startIndex: n - 1, endIndex: n } },
      })),
    },
  });
  invalidate(key);
  return hits.length;
}

module.exports = {
  init, readAll, readCached, appendRow, appendPhoto, getPhoto, readQuiz, appendQuiz, findFreeCode, markCodeUsed,
  getProgressCell, saveProgress, readLinks, saveLinks, readVideos, saveVideos, HEADERS, STATUSES,
  EDITOR_TABS, tabRows, tabRow, writeTabCells, appendTabRows, deleteTabRow, deleteTabRowsByReg, invalidate, sheetUrl,
};
```

#### `admin-database.js`

```js
// The editor dashboard's "Database" view: browse, search, add, edit and delete the records behind the website
// (students, mentorship codes, quiz results, handbook progress, site links, chapter videos) without opening
// Google Sheets. The records are stored in the Google Sheet; this file decides what may be done to each table.
//
// Every change is checked HERE, never trusted from the browser, with the same rules as the rest of the site
// (registration form, status list, grading, handbook chapters, link and video checks).
//
//   Students          create (number, sign-up time and ID date are filled in for you), edit, delete.
//                     Locked: registration number, sign-up time, mentorship code used.
//                     Deleting a student also deletes their photo, quiz results and handbook progress, and their
//                     number is never given to anyone else (new numbers go past every number ever recorded).
//   Mentorship codes  create (many at once), edit, delete. A code that a student used cannot be renamed.
//   Quiz results      create (for a registered student), edit score and total (percent and grade follow), delete.
//   Handbook progress create (one row per registered student), edit the chapters read (count and percent follow), delete.
//   Site links, Chapter videos   fixed rows (the website looks them up by name): edit the Value only.
// The Photos tab is never listed.
//
// A row is only changed when it still matches what the editor was looking at (`original`). If someone else
// changed or removed it in the meantime, the change is refused and the editor reloads, so a stale screen can
// never write over the wrong row.

const countries = require("./countries");
const states = require("./states.json");
const idrules = require("./idrules");
const grading = require("./grading");
const progress = require("./handbook-progress");
const handbook = require("./handbook-content");
const links = require("./links");
const videos = require("./videos");

const GENDERS = ["Male", "Female"];
const REG_RE = /^ABECFX\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/;
const CODE_RE = /^[A-Z0-9_-]{4,40}$/;
const MAX_CODES_AT_ONCE = 200;
const MAX_CELL = 5000;

class AdminError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, fields) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.fields = fields || null; // { columnIndex: message }
  }
}

// One line, no control characters, trimmed and capped.
function clean(v, max = 120) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v)
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .slice(0, max);
}

function lagosTimestamp() {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", { timeZone: "Africa/Lagos", dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "medium" }).format(new Date());
}

function realDate(v) {
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(v);
  if (!m) return false;
  const y = +m[1], mo = +m[2], d = +m[3];
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
}

const normReg = (v) => clean(v, 20).toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");
const same = (a, b) => String(a == null ? "" : a).trim() === String(b == null ? "" : b).trim();

function create(store) {
  const rowsOf = async (key) => (await store.tabRows(key)).map((r) => r.cells);
  const findStudent = async (reg) => (await rowsOf("students")).find((r) => (r[0] || "").toUpperCase() === reg);

  const TABS = {
    students: {
      label: "Students",
      help: "One row per student. The registration number, sign-up time and mentorship code are locked. A student's photo can only be added when the student registers.",
      editable: [2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 10, 11, 12, 13],
      selects: { 2: countries, 7: GENDERS, 9: store.STATUSES },
      placeholders: { 10: "e.g. 15 Oct 2026", 11: "YYYY-MM-DD (a new date renews an expired ID)", 13: "YYYY-MM-DD" },
      newestFirst: true,
      needsRows: true,
      create: {
        label: "Add student",
        help: "The registration number, sign-up time and ID issue date are filled in for you. A student added here has no photo, so the ID card shows their initials.",
        fields: [2, 12, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 13, 9, 10],
        defaults: { 3: "Forex Mentorship", 9: "Uncertified" },
      },
      deleteWarning: "This also deletes the student's photo, quiz results and handbook progress, and cannot be undone. Their registration number will never be given to anyone else.",
      check(p, cur, rows) {
        const errors = {};
        const changed = (i) => p[i] !== cur[i];
        if (changed(2)) {
          p[2] = clean(p[2]);
          if (!countries.includes(p[2])) errors[2] = "Choose a country from the list.";
        }
        if (changed(2) || changed(12)) {
          p[12] = clean(p[12], 60);
          if (!errors[2]) {
            const list = states[p[2]];
            if (list) {
              if (!list.includes(p[12])) errors[12] = "Choose a state or region that belongs to " + p[2] + ".";
            } else if (p[12].length < 2) {
              errors[12] = "Enter the state, province or region.";
            }
          }
        }
        if (changed(3)) {
          p[3] = clean(p[3]);
          if (!p[3]) errors[3] = "Enter the programme.";
        }
        if (changed(4)) {
          p[4] = clean(p[4]);
          if (p[4].length < 3 || !/\s/.test(p[4])) errors[4] = "Enter the full name (first and last name).";
        }
        if (changed(5)) {
          p[5] = clean(p[5], 254).toLowerCase();
          if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(p[5])) errors[5] = "Enter a valid email address.";
          else if (rows.some((r) => (r[5] || "").toLowerCase() === p[5])) errors[5] = "Another student already uses this email address.";
        }
        if (changed(6)) {
          p[6] = clean(p[6], 25);
          const digits = p[6].replace(/[^\d]/g, "");
          if (!/^\+?[\d\s\-()]+$/.test(p[6]) || digits.length < 7 || digits.length > 15) {
            errors[6] = "Enter a WhatsApp number with country code, e.g. +234 801 234 5678.";
          }
        }
        if (changed(7)) {
          p[7] = clean(p[7]);
          if (!GENDERS.includes(p[7])) errors[7] = "Choose Male or Female.";
        }
        if (changed(9)) {
          p[9] = clean(p[9]);
          if (!store.STATUSES.includes(p[9])) errors[9] = "Choose a status from the list.";
        }
        if (changed(10)) p[10] = clean(p[10], 40);
        if (changed(11)) {
          p[11] = clean(p[11], 10);
          if (p[11] && !(realDate(p[11]) && p[11] >= "2000-01-01" && p[11] <= "2100-12-31")) errors[11] = "Enter a real date as YYYY-MM-DD, or leave it empty.";
        }
        if (changed(13)) {
          p[13] = clean(p[13], 10);
          if (p[13] && !(realDate(p[13]) && p[13] >= "1920-01-01" && p[13] <= idrules.lagosToday())) {
            errors[13] = "Enter a real date of birth as YYYY-MM-DD (not in the future), or leave it empty.";
          }
        }
        return errors;
      },
      // Fill in what the website normally fills in, then return the new row.
      async build(p) {
        const [students, codes] = await Promise.all([rowsOf("students"), rowsOf("codes")]);
        const yy = idrules.lagosToday().slice(2, 4);
        const prefix = `ABECFX/${yy}/`;
        let max = 0;
        // Past every number ever recorded (students AND the codes' "used by"), so a deleted student's number is never reused.
        for (const reg of students.map((r) => r[0]).concat(codes.map((r) => r[2]))) {
          const r = String(reg || "").trim().toUpperCase();
          if (r.startsWith(prefix)) { const n = parseInt(r.slice(prefix.length), 10); if (n > max) max = n; }
        }
        if (max >= 9999) throw new AdminError(400, "This year's registration numbers are used up.");
        p[0] = prefix + String(max + 1).padStart(4, "0");
        p[1] = lagosTimestamp();
        p[8] = "";
        p[11] = p[11] || idrules.lagosToday();
        if (p[9] !== "Uncertified" && !p[10]) p[10] = idrules.formatISO(idrules.lagosToday());
        return p;
      },
      async cascade(row) {
        const reg = (row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
        if (!reg) return;
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("photos", reg, 0);
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("quiz", reg, 1);
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("progress", reg, 0);
      },
    },

    codes: {
      label: "Mentorship codes",
      help: "A code works once. A code that a student has used cannot be renamed.",
      editable: [0, 2, 3],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 2: "ABECFX/26/0001 (empty = not used)", 3: "e.g. 15 Oct 2026, 10:30:00" },
      newestFirst: true,
      needsRows: true,
      create: { label: "Add codes", mode: "text", help: "Type or paste new codes: one per line, or separated by commas. Letters, numbers, - and _ only, 4 to 40 characters. Letters are made capital." },
      deleteWarning: "If a student used this code, the record of who used it is lost. The student keeps their account.",
      check(p, cur, rows) {
        const errors = {};
        if (p[0] !== cur[0]) {
          p[0] = clean(p[0], 40).toUpperCase();
          if ((cur[2] || "").trim()) errors[0] = "This code has already been used, so its name cannot be changed.";
          else if (!CODE_RE.test(p[0])) errors[0] = "Use 4 to 40 letters, numbers, - or _.";
          else if (rows.some((r) => (r[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === p[0])) errors[0] = "This code is already in the list.";
        }
        if (p[2] !== cur[2]) {
          p[2] = clean(p[2], 20).toUpperCase();
          if (p[2] && !REG_RE.test(p[2])) errors[2] = "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001, or leave it empty.";
        }
        if (p[3] !== cur[3]) p[3] = clean(p[3], 40);
        if (!p[2]) p[3] = ""; // an unused code has no "used at" time
        return errors;
      },
      // text -> rows to append
      prepareText(text, rows) {
        const wanted = String(text == null ? "" : text).split(/[\s,;]+/).map((c) => c.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
        if (!wanted.length) throw new AdminError(400, "Type at least one code.");
        if (wanted.length > MAX_CODES_AT_ONCE) throw new AdminError(400, "Add at most " + MAX_CODES_AT_ONCE + " codes at a time.");
        const bad = wanted.filter((c) => !CODE_RE.test(c));
        if (bad.length) throw new AdminError(400, "These are not valid codes (use 4 to 40 letters, numbers, - or _): " + bad.slice(0, 5).join(", ") + (bad.length > 5 ? "..." : ""));
        const have = new Set(rows.map((r) => (r[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase()));
        const seen = new Set();
        const dup = [];
        for (const c of wanted) {
          if (have.has(c) || seen.has(c)) dup.push(c);
          seen.add(c);
        }
        if (dup.length) throw new AdminError(400, "Already in the list or typed twice: " + [...new Set(dup)].slice(0, 5).join(", ") + (dup.length > 5 ? "..." : ""));
        const at = lagosTimestamp();
        return [...seen].map((c) => [c, at, "", ""]);
      },
    },

    quiz: {
      label: "Quiz results",
      help: "Every finished quiz attempt. Edit the score or total and the percent and grade follow. Adding a result needs a registered student.",
      editable: [3, 4],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 3: "Questions answered correctly", 4: "Number of questions in the quiz" },
      newestFirst: true,
      canDelete: true,
      create: { label: "Add result", help: "Enter the student's registration number, the score and the total. The name, percent, grade and attempt number are worked out for you.", fields: [1, 3, 4] },
      deleteWarning: "This quiz result is removed for good.",
      check(p) {
        const errors = {};
        const score = Number(p[3]), total = Number(p[4]);
        if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(clean(p[3], 4)) || !Number.isInteger(score)) errors[3] = "Enter the score as a whole number.";
        if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(clean(p[4], 4)) || !Number.isInteger(total) || total < 1 || total > 500) errors[4] = "Enter the total number of questions (1 to 500).";
        if (!errors[3] && !errors[4] && score > total) errors[3] = "The score cannot be more than the total.";
        if (Object.keys(errors).length) return errors;
        const pct = grading.percent(score, total);
        p[3] = String(score); p[4] = String(total); p[5] = String(pct); p[6] = grading.gradeFor(pct).grade;
        return {};
      },
      async prepare(p) {
        const reg = normReg(p[1]);
        if (!REG_RE.test(reg)) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 1: "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001." });
        const stu = await findStudent(reg);
        if (!stu) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 1: "No student has this registration number." });
        const errors = this.check(p, []);
        if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
        const mine = (await rowsOf("quiz")).filter((q) => (q[1] || "").toUpperCase() === reg);
        return [lagosTimestamp(), reg, stu[4] || "", p[3], p[4], p[5], p[6], String(mine.length + 1), "admin", "{}", new Date().toISOString()];
      },
    },

    progress: {
      label: "Handbook progress",
      help: "Which handbook chapters each student has marked as read (00 is the introduction, 01 to 12 the chapters). Count and percent follow the chapters you list.",
      editable: [2],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 2: "e.g. 00, 01, 02" },
      newestFirst: false,
      canDelete: true,
      create: { label: "Add progress", help: "Choose a registered student who has no progress row yet, and list the chapters read, e.g. 00, 01, 02. Leave it empty for none.", fields: [0, 2] },
      deleteWarning: "This resets the student's handbook progress to nothing.",
      check(p) {
        const tokens = String(p[2] == null ? "" : p[2]).split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean);
        const bad = tokens.filter((t) => !handbook.IDS.includes(/^\d{1,2}$/.test(t) ? t.padStart(2, "0") : t));
        if (bad.length) return { 2: "Not a chapter: " + bad.slice(0, 5).join(", ") + ". Use " + handbook.IDS[0] + " to " + handbook.IDS[handbook.IDS.length - 1] + "." };
        const sum = progress.summary(progress.parseRead(tokens.join(",")));
        p[2] = sum.read.join(",");
        p[3] = String(sum.count); p[4] = String(sum.total); p[5] = String(sum.percent); p[6] = lagosTimestamp();
        return {};
      },
      async prepare(p) {
        const reg = normReg(p[0]);
        if (!REG_RE.test(reg)) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001." });
        const stu = await findStudent(reg);
        if (!stu) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "No student has this registration number." });
        if ((await rowsOf("progress")).some((r) => (r[0] || "").toUpperCase() === reg)) {
          throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "This student already has a progress row. Edit that row instead." });
        }
        const row = [reg, stu[4] || "", p[2] || "", "", "", "", ""];
        const e = this.check(row); // fills in count, total, percent and time
        if (Object.keys(e).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 2: e[2] });
        return row;
      },
    },

    links: {
      label: "Site links",
      help: "The icons and text shown on the website. The rows are fixed (the website looks them up by name), so only the Value can be changed, with the same checks as the Site links tab.",
      editable: [2],
      selects: {},
      multiline: [2],
      newestFirst: false,
      check(p, cur) {
        const key = (cur[0] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!links.FIELDS.some((f) => f.key === key)) return { 2: "This row is not one of the website's links, so it cannot be changed here." };
        const out = links.sanitize({ [key]: p[2] });
        if (out.errors[key]) return { 2: out.errors[key] };
        p[2] = out.values[key];
        return {};
      },
    },

    videos: {
      label: "Chapter videos",
      help: "The YouTube video for each handbook chapter. The rows are fixed (one per chapter), so only the address can be changed, with the same checks as the Chapter videos tab.",
      editable: [2],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 2: "https://youtu.be/..." },
      newestFirst: false,
      check(p, cur) {
        const key = (cur[0] || "").trim().padStart(2, "0");
        if (!videos.VIDEO_CHAPTERS.some((c) => c.id === key)) return { 2: "This row is not one of the handbook chapters, so it cannot be changed here." };
        const out = videos.sanitize({ [key]: p[2] });
        if (out.errors[key]) return { 2: out.errors[key] };
        p[2] = out.values[key];
        return {};
      },
    },
  };

  // Students first: they are what most editing is about.
  const ORDER = ["students", "codes", "quiz", "progress", "links", "videos"];

  function tabOf(key) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(TABS, key)) throw new AdminError(404, "That table could not be found.");
    return TABS[key];
  }

  function rowNumber(n) {
    const v = Number(n);
    if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 2 || v > 1000000) throw new AdminError(400, "That row could not be found.");
    return v;
  }

  function describe(key) {
    const t = TABS[key];
    const c = t.create;
    return {
      key,
      label: t.label,
      help: t.help,
      headers: store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers,
      editable: t.editable,
      selects: t.selects || {},
      multiline: t.multiline || [],
      placeholders: t.placeholders || {},
      add: c ? { label: c.label, help: c.help, mode: c.mode || "form", fields: c.fields || [], defaults: c.defaults || {} } : null,
      canDelete: Boolean(t.canDelete || t.cascade || t.deleteWarning),
      deleteWarning: t.deleteWarning || "",
      newestFirst: Boolean(t.newestFirst),
    };
  }

  function assertUnchanged(current, original, width) {
    const ok = Array.isArray(original) && current.length === width && current.every((c, i) => same(c, original[i]));
    if (!ok) {
      throw new AdminError(409, "This row was changed or removed since you opened it. The latest version has been loaded.");
    }
  }

  async function otherRows(key, skipN) {
    return (await store.tabRows(key)).filter((r) => r.n !== skipN).map((r) => r.cells);
  }

  return {
    TABS,
    ORDER,

    // What the dashboard needs to draw the table picker and the forms.
    meta() {
      return { sheets: ORDER.map(describe), sheetUrl: store.sheetUrl ? store.sheetUrl() : "" };
    },

    async read(key) {
      tabOf(key);
      return { ...describe(key), rows: await store.tabRows(key) };
    },

    async update(key, n, original, values) {
      const t = tabOf(key);
      if (!t.editable.length) throw new AdminError(403, "This table cannot be edited here.");
      n = rowNumber(n);
      const width = store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers.length;
      const current = await store.tabRow(key, n);
      assertUnchanged(current, original, width);

      const p = current.slice();
      const errors = {};
      for (const [k, raw] of Object.entries(values && typeof values === "object" ? values : {})) {
        const i = Number(k);
        if (!Number.isInteger(i) || i < 0 || i >= width) continue;
        const v = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).slice(0, MAX_CELL);
        if (t.editable.includes(i)) p[i] = v;
        else if (!same(v, current[i])) errors[i] = "This column is locked and cannot be changed here.";
      }
      if (!Object.keys(errors).length) {
        Object.assign(errors, t.check(p, current, t.needsRows ? await otherRows(key, n) : []));
      }
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);

      const changes = {};
      p.forEach((v, i) => { if (v !== current[i]) changes[i] = v; });
      if (!Object.keys(changes).length) return { row: current, changed: false };
      await store.writeTabCells(key, n, changes);
      return { row: p, changed: true };
    },

    // body: { text } for the codes table, { values: { columnIndex: text } } for everything else.
    async create(key, body) {
      const t = tabOf(key);
      const c = t.create;
      if (!c) throw new AdminError(403, "Rows cannot be added to this table here.");
      const b = body && typeof body === "object" ? body : {};
      if (c.mode === "text") {
        const rows = t.prepareText(b.text, await otherRows(key, -1));
        await store.appendTabRows(key, rows);
        return { added: rows.length };
      }
      const width = store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers.length;
      const p = Array(width).fill("");
      Object.entries(c.defaults || {}).forEach(([i, v]) => { p[Number(i)] = v; });
      for (const [k, raw] of Object.entries(b.values && typeof b.values === "object" ? b.values : {})) {
        const i = Number(k);
        if (!c.fields.includes(i)) continue; // anything the form does not offer is ignored
        const v = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).slice(0, MAX_CELL);
        if (v.trim() || !(c.defaults && c.defaults[i])) p[i] = v;
      }
      let row;
      if (t.prepare) {
        row = await t.prepare(p);
      } else {
        // every column counts as "changed" when creating, so all of them are checked
        const errors = t.check(p, Array(width).fill(null), t.needsRows ? await otherRows(key, -1) : []);
        if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
        row = await t.build(p);
      }
      await store.appendTabRows(key, [row]);
      return { added: 1, row, ...(key === "students" ? { reg: row[0] } : {}) };
    },

    async remove(key, n, original) {
      const t = tabOf(key);
      if (!(t.canDelete || t.cascade || t.deleteWarning)) throw new AdminError(403, "Rows cannot be deleted from this table here.");
      n = rowNumber(n);
      const width = store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers.length;
      const current = await store.tabRow(key, n);
      assertUnchanged(current, original, width);
      if (t.cascade) await t.cascade(current);
      await store.deleteTabRow(key, n);
      return { ok: true };
    },
  };
}

module.exports = { create, AdminError };
```

#### `idrules.js`

```js
// ID card rules: who gets an expiry date, and the date maths behind it.
// Certified students are graduates. Only Uncertified students (not yet graduates) have IDs that are
// valid for 2 months from the issue date. Certified, Graduate, Alumni Member, Mentor, Staff Member
// and CEO have no expiry.

const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
const EXPIRING_STATUSES = ["Uncertified"];
const VALID_MONTHS = 2;
const ISO = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

// Today's date in Lagos as YYYY-MM-DD.
function lagosToday(now = new Date()) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: "Africa/Lagos" }).format(now);
}

// Issue date as YYYY-MM-DD. Uses the "ID Issue Date" column when it holds one; otherwise reads the
// "13 Oct 2026, 14:20:11" text in "Registered At", so students registered before this column existed still work.
function parseIssued(row) {
  const explicit = String(row[11] || "").trim();
  if (ISO.test(explicit)) return explicit;
  return parseRegistered(row);
}

// Date the student joined, as YYYY-MM-DD, read from "Registered At" ("13 Oct 2026, 14:20:11").
// Unlike the ID issue date, this never changes when an editor renews an ID.
function parseRegistered(row) {
  const m = /^(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z]{3,})\.?\s+(\d{4})/.exec(String(row[1] || "").trim());
  if (m) {
    const mi = MONTHS.findIndex((x) => x.toLowerCase() === m[2].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase());
    if (mi >= 0) return `${m[3]}-${pad(mi + 1)}-${pad(Number(m[1]))}`;
  }
  return "";
}

// Add whole months, keeping the day of month where possible (31 Dec + 2 months = 28/29 Feb).
function addMonthsISO(iso, n) {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
  const t = m - 1 + n;
  const ny = y + Math.floor(t / 12);
  const nm = ((t % 12) + 12) % 12;
  const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(ny, nm + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
  return `${ny}-${pad(nm + 1)}-${pad(Math.min(d, lastDay))}`;
}

function formatISO(iso) {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
  return `${pad(d)} ${MONTHS[m - 1]} ${y}`;
}

function daysBetween(fromIso, toIso) {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(toIso + "T00:00:00Z") - Date.parse(fromIso + "T00:00:00Z")) / 86400000);
}

// expiryKind: "none" (never expires), "date" (has an expiry date), "unknown" (should expire but no issue date).
// An ID is valid through its expiry date and counts as expired from the day after.
function expiryInfo(status, issuedISO, todayISO) {
  if (!EXPIRING_STATUSES.includes(status)) {
    return { expiryKind: "none", expires: "", expired: false, daysLeft: null };
  }
  if (!issuedISO) return { expiryKind: "unknown", expires: "", expired: false, daysLeft: null };
  const exp = addMonthsISO(issuedISO, VALID_MONTHS);
  const daysLeft = daysBetween(todayISO, exp);
  return { expiryKind: "date", expires: formatISO(exp), expired: daysLeft < 0, daysLeft };
}

module.exports = { lagosToday, parseIssued, parseRegistered, addMonthsISO, formatISO, daysBetween, expiryInfo, EXPIRING_STATUSES };
```

#### `grading.js`

```js
// A-F grading scale for quiz results. Change the numbers here and the whole site follows.
// Scale used (common Nigerian grading): A 70-100, B 60-69, C 50-59, D 45-49, E 40-44, F 0-39.
const SCALE = [
  { grade: "A", min: 70, label: "Excellent" },
  { grade: "B", min: 60, label: "Very good" },
  { grade: "C", min: 50, label: "Good" },
  { grade: "D", min: 45, label: "Fair" },
  { grade: "E", min: 40, label: "Pass" },
  { grade: "F", min: 0, label: "Fail" },
];

// pct is the whole-number percentage that is shown to the student (0-100).
function gradeFor(pct) {
  const p = Number.isFinite(pct) ? pct : 0;
  return SCALE.find((s) => p >= s.min) || SCALE[SCALE.length - 1];
}

function percent(score, total) {
  return total > 0 ? Math.round((score / total) * 100) : 0;
}

module.exports = { SCALE, gradeFor, percent };
```

#### `stats.js`

```js
// Statistics for the editor dashboard. Pure functions: rows in, numbers out.
// Student row columns: 0 reg, 1 registered at, 2 country, 3 programme, 4 name, 7 gender, 9 status, 12 state.
// Quiz row columns: 0 submitted at, 1 reg, 2 name, 3 score, 4 total, 5 percent, 6 grade, 7 attempt.
const idrules = require("./idrules");
const grading = require("./grading");

const GRADUATE_STATUSES = ["Certified", "Graduate", "Alumni Member"];
const TEAM_STATUSES = ["Mentor", "Staff Member", "CEO"];
const STATUS_ORDER = ["Uncertified", "Certified", "Graduate", "Alumni Member", "Mentor", "Staff Member", "CEO"];

// The label each student is counted under, for every breakdown. Used by the counts AND by the lists
// behind them, so a number on the dashboard and the list it opens can never disagree.
const BLANK = "Not recorded";
const KEYS = {
  gender: (r) => r[7],
  country: (r) => r[2],
  programme: (r) => r[3],
  state: (r) => (r[12] ? `${r[12]}${r[2] ? ", " + r[2] : ""}` : ""),
  year: (r) => {
    // year joined = the registration number's year, which is the admission year
    const yy = parseInt(String(r[0] || "").split("/")[1], 10);
    return Number.isNaN(yy) ? "" : String(2000 + yy);
  },
};
const labelOf = (kind, r) => (KEYS[kind](r) || "").trim() || BLANK;

function tally(values, blank = BLANK) {
  const m = new Map();
  for (const v of values) {
    const k = (v || "").trim() || blank;
    m.set(k, (m.get(k) || 0) + 1);
  }
  return [...m.entries()]
    .map(([label, count]) => ({ label, count }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count || a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
}

function ymOf(iso) {
  return iso.slice(0, 7);
}

function nextYm(ym) {
  let [y, m] = ym.split("-").map(Number);
  m += 1;
  if (m > 12) { m = 1; y += 1; }
  return `${y}-${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function statusOf(r) {
  return STATUS_ORDER.includes(r[9]) ? r[9] : "Uncertified";
}

function buildStats(rows, quizRows, todayISO) {
  const total = rows.length;
  const statusCounts = Object.fromEntries(STATUS_ORDER.map((s) => [s, 0]));
  for (const r of rows) statusCounts[statusOf(r)] += 1;
  const sum = (list) => list.reduce((n, s) => n + statusCounts[s], 0);

  // ---- joining dates ----
  const joined = rows.map((r) => idrules.parseRegistered(r)).filter(Boolean);
  const undated = total - joined.length;
  const perMonth = new Map();
  for (const iso of joined) perMonth.set(ymOf(iso), (perMonth.get(ymOf(iso)) || 0) + 1);

  const months = [];
  if (perMonth.size) {
    const sorted = [...perMonth.keys()].sort();
    const last = [sorted[sorted.length - 1], ymOf(todayISO)].sort().pop();
    let running = 0;
    let prevNew = null;
    for (let ym = sorted[0]; ym <= last; ym = nextYm(ym)) {
      const n = perMonth.get(ym) || 0;
      running += n;
      months.push({
        ym,
        new: n,
        total: running,
        growthPct: prevNew === null || prevNew === 0 ? null : Math.round(((n - prevNew) / prevNew) * 100),
      });
      prevNew = n;
    }
  }
  const monthsByYear = {};
  for (const m of months) {
    const [y, mo] = m.ym.split("-");
    (monthsByYear[y] = monthsByYear[y] || Array(12).fill(0))[Number(mo) - 1] = m.new;
  }

  // year joined: the registration number's year, which is the admission year
  const byYear = tally(rows.map(KEYS.year)).sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label));

  // ---- quiz ----
  const latest = new Map(); // reg -> most recent attempt
  const best = new Map();
  for (const q of quizRows) {
    const reg = q[1];
    const pct = Number(q[5]) || 0;
    latest.set(reg, q);
    if (!best.has(reg) || pct > (Number(best.get(reg)[5]) || 0)) best.set(reg, q);
  }
  const latestList = [...latest.values()];
  const gradeDist = grading.SCALE.map((s) => ({
    grade: s.grade,
    label: s.label,
    count: latestList.filter((q) => q[6] === s.grade).length,
  }));
  const avgPct = latestList.length
    ? Math.round(latestList.reduce((n, q) => n + (Number(q[5]) || 0), 0) / latestList.length)
    : null;

  return {
    total,
    gender: tally(rows.map(KEYS.gender)),
    country: tally(rows.map(KEYS.country)),
    state: tally(rows.map(KEYS.state)),
    programme: tally(rows.map(KEYS.programme)),
    status: STATUS_ORDER.map((s) => ({ label: s, count: statusCounts[s] })),
    graduates: sum(GRADUATE_STATUSES), // Certified + Graduate + Alumni Member
    uncertified: statusCounts.Uncertified,
    team: sum(TEAM_STATUSES), // Mentor + Staff Member + CEO
    byYear,
    months,
    monthsByYear,
    undated,
    quiz: {
      attempts: quizRows.length,
      students: latest.size,
      notTaken: rows.filter((r) => !latest.has(r[0])).length, // students with no quiz result at all
      avgPct,
      gradeDist,
    },
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// The list behind a number: click a statistic on the dashboard, get these rows.
// No email, WhatsApp number, photo or mentorship code is ever included.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const MAX_ROWS = 5000;
const YM = /^\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/;
const MONTH_NAMES = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];

function quizEntry(q) {
  const g = grading.gradeFor(Number(q[5]));
  return {
    date: (q[0] || "").split(",")[0],
    regNumber: q[1] || "",
    fullName: q[2] || "",
    score: Number(q[3]) || 0,
    total: Number(q[4]) || 0,
    pct: Number(q[5]) || 0,
    grade: q[6] || g.grade,
    label: g.label,
    attempt: Number(q[7]) || 1,
  };
}

function studentOut(r) {
  const iso = idrules.parseRegistered(r);
  return {
    regNumber: r[0] || "",
    fullName: r[4] || "",
    gender: r[7] || "",
    country: r[2] || "",
    state: r[12] || "",
    programme: r[3] || "",
    status: statusOf(r),
    joined: iso ? idrules.formatISO(iso) : "",
  };
}

function monthTitle(ym) {
  const [y, m] = ym.split("-");
  return `${MONTH_NAMES[Number(m) - 1]} ${y}`;
}

// kind: which statistic; value: which bar / column / grade (for the kinds that need one).
// Returns { title, type: "students" | "quiz", rows, truncated, count } or null when kind/value is not valid.
function drill(rows, quizRows, kind, value) {
  const val = String(value == null ? "" : value).slice(0, 120);
  let title, pick, type = "students";

  const latest = new Map();
  for (const q of quizRows) latest.set(q[1], q);
  const latestList = () => [...latest.values()].sort((a, b) => (Number(b[5]) || 0) - (Number(a[5]) || 0) || String(a[1]).localeCompare(String(b[1])));

  switch (kind) {
    case "all": title = "All students"; pick = () => true; break;
    case "graduates": title = "Certified / graduates (Certified, Graduate and Alumni Member)"; pick = (r) => GRADUATE_STATUSES.includes(statusOf(r)); break;
    case "uncertified": title = "Uncertified students"; pick = (r) => statusOf(r) === "Uncertified"; break;
    case "team": title = "Mentors, staff and CEO"; pick = (r) => TEAM_STATUSES.includes(statusOf(r)); break;
    case "status":
      if (!STATUS_ORDER.includes(val)) return null;
      title = `Status: ${val}`; pick = (r) => statusOf(r) === val; break;
    case "gender": case "country": case "state": case "programme": case "year": {
      if (!val) return null;
      const name = { gender: "Gender", country: "Country", state: "State", programme: "Programme", year: "Year joined" }[kind];
      title = `${name}: ${val}`; pick = (r) => labelOf(kind, r) === val; break;
    }
    case "month":
      if (!YM.test(val)) return null;
      title = `Joined in ${monthTitle(val)}`; pick = (r) => (idrules.parseRegistered(r) || "").slice(0, 7) === val; break;
    case "upto":
      if (!YM.test(val)) return null;
      title = `All students who had joined by the end of ${monthTitle(val)}`;
      pick = (r) => { const m = (idrules.parseRegistered(r) || "").slice(0, 7); return !!m && m <= val; }; break;
    case "undated": title = "Students with no joining date"; pick = (r) => !idrules.parseRegistered(r); break;
    case "quiz-not-taken": title = "Students who have not taken the quiz yet"; pick = (r) => !latest.has(r[0]); break;
    case "quiz-students": case "quiz-avg": {
      type = "quiz";
      title = kind === "quiz-avg" ? "Average quiz score: each student's latest attempt" : "Students who took the quiz (latest attempt)";
      const list = latestList().map(quizEntry);
      return finish(title, type, list);
    }
    case "grade": {
      if (!grading.SCALE.some((s) => s.grade === val)) return null;
      type = "quiz";
      title = `Grade ${val} (latest attempt per student)`;
      return finish(title, type, latestList().filter((q) => q[6] === val).map(quizEntry));
    }
    case "quiz-attempts": {
      type = "quiz";
      return finish("Every quiz attempt (newest first)", type, quizRows.map(quizEntry).reverse());
    }
    default: return null;
  }
  const list = rows.filter(pick).map(studentOut).sort((a, b) => a.regNumber.localeCompare(b.regNumber));
  return finish(title, type, list);
}

function finish(title, type, list) {
  return { title, type, count: list.length, truncated: list.length > MAX_ROWS, rows: list.slice(0, MAX_ROWS) };
}

module.exports = { buildStats, drill, quizEntry, GRADUATE_STATUSES, TEAM_STATUSES };
```

#### `keepalive.js`

```js
// Keeps a Render free instance from going idle: while the app is running it requests its own public
// /healthz address every 10 minutes. Render counts that as traffic, so the 15-minute idle timer resets.
// It cannot wake a sleeping service, so pair it with an outside pinger (see README).
// Render sets RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL automatically. Set KEEP_AWAKE=off to turn this off.
function startKeepAlive({ intervalMs = 10 * 60 * 1000, url = process.env.RENDER_EXTERNAL_URL, log = console } = {}) {
  if (process.env.KEEP_AWAKE === "off" || !url) return null;
  const target = url.replace(/\/+$/, "") + "/healthz";
  const ping = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(target, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000) });
      if (!res.ok) log.error("Keep-alive ping got status", res.status);
    } catch (e) {
      log.error("Keep-alive ping failed:", e.message);
    }
  };
  const timer = setInterval(ping, intervalMs);
  timer.unref();
  return { timer, ping, target };
}

module.exports = { startKeepAlive };
```

#### `quiztoken.js`

```js
// Short-lived "quiz pass" for the in-portal quiz.
// The website signs a pass for one registration number; the quiz service (which holds the same
// QUIZ_API_KEY) checks the signature. The pass cannot be changed or reused for another number.
const crypto = require("crypto");

const TTL_MS = 4 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // a full quiz is long; 4 hours is plenty

function mac(payload, secret) {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update("portal." + payload).digest("base64url");
}

function sign(reg, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ r: reg, e: now + TTL_MS })).toString("base64url");
  return payload + "." + mac(payload, secret);
}

// Returns the registration number, or null when the pass is wrong, tampered with or expired.
function verify(token, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  if (typeof token !== "string" || token.length > 600 || !secret) return null;
  const [payload, sig, extra] = token.split(".");
  if (!payload || !sig || extra !== undefined) return null;
  const a = Buffer.from(mac(payload, secret));
  const b = Buffer.from(sig);
  if (a.length !== b.length || !crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null;
  try {
    const d = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
    if (!/^ABECFX\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/.test(d.r) || !(d.e > now)) return null;
    return d.r;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

module.exports = { sign, verify, TTL_MS };
```

#### `session.js`

```js
// Student portal sign-in session.
// After a student proves who they are (registration number + registered email) the website hands
// back a signed session token. The portal sends it with every private request. It cannot be
// changed or used for another registration number, and it expires after 30 days.
const crypto = require("crypto");

const TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function mac(payload, secret) {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update("session." + payload).digest("base64url");
}

function sign(reg, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ r: reg, e: now + TTL_MS })).toString("base64url");
  return payload + "." + mac(payload, secret);
}

// Returns the registration number, or null when the token is wrong, tampered with or expired.
function verify(token, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  if (typeof token !== "string" || token.length > 600 || !secret) return null;
  const [payload, sig, extra] = token.split(".");
  if (!payload || !sig || extra !== undefined) return null;
  const a = Buffer.from(mac(payload, secret));
  const b = Buffer.from(sig);
  if (a.length !== b.length || !crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null;
  try {
    const d = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
    if (!/^ABECFX\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/.test(d.r) || !(d.e > now)) return null;
    return d.r;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

module.exports = { sign, verify, TTL_MS };
```

#### `handbook-progress.js`

```js
// A student's learning progress through the Mentorship Handbook.
// Progress = chapters marked as read / total chapters. The quiz is separate and does not count.
const { IDS } = require("./handbook-content");

// "00, 01,3" -> ["00","01","03"]. Tolerates hand edits in the sheet (numbers typed without a leading zero).
function parseRead(cell) {
  const out = new Set();
  String(cell == null ? "" : cell)
    .split(/[\s,;]+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .forEach((t) => {
      const id = /^\d{1,2}$/.test(t) ? t.padStart(2, "0") : t;
      if (IDS.includes(id)) out.add(id);
    });
  return IDS.filter((id) => out.has(id)); // always in handbook order, never an unknown id
}

function summary(readIds) {
  const read = IDS.filter((id) => readIds.includes(id));
  const total = IDS.length;
  return { read, count: read.length, total, percent: total ? Math.round((read.length / total) * 100) : 0 };
}

// Returns the new list of read chapter ids, or null if the chapter id is not a real chapter.
function setRead(readIds, chapterId, isRead) {
  if (!IDS.includes(chapterId)) return null;
  const set = new Set(readIds);
  if (isRead) set.add(chapterId); else set.delete(chapterId);
  return IDS.filter((id) => set.has(id));
}

module.exports = { parseRead, summary, setRead };
```

#### `pdflink.js`

```js
// Short-lived download link for the handbook PDF.
// The PDF is not in the public folder. A signed-in student asks for a link, the website signs one that
// works for 10 minutes, and /handbook.pdf only serves the file when the link's signature is valid.
const crypto = require("crypto");

const TTL_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000;

function mac(payload, secret) {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update("handbookpdf." + payload).digest("base64url");
}

function sign(reg, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ r: reg, e: now + TTL_MS })).toString("base64url");
  return payload + "." + mac(payload, secret);
}

// Returns the registration number, or null when the link is wrong, changed or expired.
function verify(token, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  if (typeof token !== "string" || token.length > 600 || !secret) return null;
  const [payload, sig, extra] = token.split(".");
  if (!payload || !sig || extra !== undefined) return null;
  const a = Buffer.from(mac(payload, secret));
  const b = Buffer.from(sig);
  if (a.length !== b.length || !crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null;
  try {
    const d = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
    if (!/^ABECFX\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/.test(d.r) || !(d.e > now)) return null;
    return d.r;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

module.exports = { sign, verify, TTL_MS };
```

```html
              <span class="err" data-for="gender"></span>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <span class="label">Programme</span>
              <div class="programme">
                <span class="dot" aria-hidden="true">
                  <svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M2.5 7.5l3 3 6-7"/></svg>
                </span>
                <div><strong>Forex Mentorship</strong><span>Selected programme</span></div>
              </div>
              <input type="hidden" name="programme" value="Forex Mentorship" />
              <span class="err" data-for="programme"></span>
            </div>

            <div class="field">
              <label for="mentorshipCode">Mentorship code <em>(required)</em></label>
              <input id="mentorshipCode" name="mentorshipCode" type="text" required autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" placeholder="Code given to you by your mentor" />
              <span class="err" data-for="mentorshipCode"></span>
            </div>

            <div class="hp" aria-hidden="true">
              <label>Website <input type="text" name="website" tabindex="-1" autocomplete="off" /></label>
            </div>

            <p class="formErr" id="formErr" role="alert"></p>
            <button type="submit" id="submitBtn" class="primary">Register</button>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section id="successCard" hidden>
          <div class="success-head">
            <div class="tick" aria-hidden="true">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M2.5 7.5l3 3 6-7"/></svg>
            </div>
            <h2 id="welcome">You're registered</h2>
            <p class="lead" style="margin:0">This is your student ID card. Save your registration number to check your status later.</p>
          </div>

          <div class="cardwrap" id="cardMount"></div>

          <div class="pass-actions">
            <button type="button" id="copyBtn" class="btn ghost">Copy number</button>
            <button type="button" id="printBtn" class="btn ghost">Print ID card</button>
          </div>
          <p class="note" style="text-align:center;margin-top:14px"><a id="statusLink" href="/" class="btn primary" style="text-decoration:none;max-width:360px;margin:0 auto">Go to my student portal</a></p>
        </section>

        <p class="sheet-foot">&copy; <span id="year"></span> Adebass Base Edge Consult</p>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>
  <script src="/links.js"></script>
  <script src="/qr.js"></script>
  <script src="/card.js"></script>
  <script src="/app.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

#### `public/app.js`

```js
(function () {
  var form = document.getElementById("regForm");
  var btn = document.getElementById("submitBtn");
  var formErr = document.getElementById("formErr");
  var countrySel = document.getElementById("country");

  document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

  // A student who registered on this device is taken back to their portal, not the form.
  var KEY = "abecfx_reg";
  try {
    var saved = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (saved) {
      document.getElementById("returningReg").textContent = saved;
      document.getElementById("returning").hidden = false;
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  fetch("/api/countries")
    .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
    .then(function (list) {
      list.forEach(function (c) {
        var o = document.createElement("option");
        o.value = c;
        o.textContent = c;
        countrySel.appendChild(o);
      });
    })
    .catch(function () {
      formErr.textContent = "Could not load the country list. Refresh the page to try again.";
    });


  // ---- State / region: appears once a country is chosen. A dropdown when we have a list, else a text box ----
  var stateField = document.getElementById("stateField");
  var stateSel = document.getElementById("state");
  var stateText = document.getElementById("stateText");
  var stateLabel = document.getElementById("stateLabel");
  var STATE_WORD = {
    "Nigeria": "State", "United States": "State", "India": "State", "Brazil": "State", "Australia": "State",
    "Mexico": "State", "Malaysia": "State", "Germany": "State", "Austria": "State", "Venezuela": "State",
    "Canada": "Province", "South Africa": "Province", "Argentina": "Province", "China": "Province",
    "Ghana": "Region", "Cameroon": "Region", "Tanzania": "Region", "Senegal": "Region", "Namibia": "Region"
  };
  var stateReq = 0;

  function useStateControl(sel) {
    stateSel.hidden = !sel;
    stateText.hidden = !!sel;
    stateLabel.setAttribute("for", sel ? "state" : "stateText");
  }

  countrySel.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var country = countrySel.value;
    var mine = ++stateReq;
    stateSel.replaceChildren();
    stateText.value = "";
    if (!country) { stateField.hidden = true; return; }
    var word = STATE_WORD[country] || "State / Region";
    stateLabel.textContent = word;
    stateText.placeholder = word === "State / Region" ? "State, province or region" : word;
    stateField.hidden = false;
    useStateControl(false); // text box until the list arrives
    fetch("/api/states?country=" + encodeURIComponent(country))
      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(function (list) {
        if (mine !== stateReq || !list.length) return;
        var first = document.createElement("option");
        first.value = ""; first.textContent = "Select your " + word.toLowerCase();
        stateSel.appendChild(first);
        list.forEach(function (s) {
          var o = document.createElement("option");
          o.value = s; o.textContent = s;
          stateSel.appendChild(o);
        });
        useStateControl(true);
      })
      .catch(function () {});
  });

  // ---- Passport photo: crop to 4:5, shrink to 320x400 JPEG so it fits in a Google Sheet cell ----
  var photoInput = document.getElementById("photo");
  var photoPick = document.getElementById("photoPick");
  var photoPreview = document.getElementById("photoPreview");
  var photoIcon = document.getElementById("photoIcon");
  var photoBtn = document.getElementById("photoBtn");
  var regNumberText = "";
  var photoB64 = "";
  var photoDataUrl = "";

  function setPhotoError(msg) {
    var span = form.querySelector('.err[data-for="photo"]');
    span.textContent = msg || "";
    span.closest(".field").classList.toggle("invalid", !!msg);
  }

  // Open the picked file as something we can draw. createImageBitmap needs no blob: address (the site's
  // security policy blocks those); the FileReader fallback uses a data: address, which is allowed.
  function openImage(file) {
    if (window.createImageBitmap) {
      return createImageBitmap(file).then(
        function (bmp) { return { src: bmp, w: bmp.width, h: bmp.height }; },
        function () { return openViaReader(file); }
      );
    }
    return openViaReader(file);
  }
  function openViaReader(file) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
      var fr = new FileReader();
      fr.onerror = function () { reject(new Error("read")); };
      fr.onload = function () {
        var img = new Image();
        img.onload = function () { resolve({ src: img, w: img.naturalWidth, h: img.naturalHeight }); };
        img.onerror = function () { reject(new Error("decode")); };
        img.src = fr.result;
      };
      fr.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  }

  function processPhoto(file) {
    if (file.type && !/^image\//.test(file.type)) return Promise.reject(new Error("Choose an image file (JPG or PNG)."));
    if (file.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) return Promise.reject(new Error("That photo is too large. Choose one under 15 MB."));
    return openImage(file).then(
      function (im) {
        var sw = im.w, sh = im.h;
        if (sw < 200 || sh < 250) throw new Error("That photo is too small. Choose a larger, clearer one.");
        // largest 4:5 area, biased toward the top so heads are not cut off
        var cw = sw, ch = sw * 1.25;
        if (ch > sh) { ch = sh; cw = sh * 0.8; }
        var sx = (sw - cw) / 2, sy = (sh - ch) * 0.25;
        var sizes = [[320, 400], [256, 320], [200, 250]];
        for (var i = 0; i < sizes.length; i++) {
          var c = document.createElement("canvas");
          c.width = sizes[i][0]; c.height = sizes[i][1];
          var ctx = c.getContext("2d");
          ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
          ctx.drawImage(im.src, sx, sy, cw, ch, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
          for (var q = 0.86; q >= 0.4; q -= 0.06) {
            var data = c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", q);
            if (data.length <= 42000) { if (im.src.close) im.src.close(); return data; }
          }
        }
        if (im.src.close) im.src.close();
        throw new Error("We could not shrink that photo. Try a different one.");
      },
      function () {
        throw new Error("We could not read that photo. Use a JPG or PNG. On iPhone, pick the photo again from your library or take a new one.");
      }
    );
  }

  photoInput.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var file = photoInput.files && photoInput.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setPhotoError("");
    processPhoto(file)
      .then(function (dataUrl) {
        photoDataUrl = dataUrl;
        photoB64 = dataUrl.slice(dataUrl.indexOf(",") + 1);
        photoPreview.src = dataUrl;
        photoPreview.hidden = false;
        photoIcon.style.display = "none";
        photoPick.classList.add("has-photo");
        photoBtn.textContent = "Change photo";
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        photoB64 = ""; photoDataUrl = "";
        photoPreview.hidden = true;
        photoIcon.style.display = "";
        photoPick.classList.remove("has-photo");
        photoBtn.textContent = "Choose photo";
        photoInput.value = "";
        setPhotoError(e.message);
      });
  });

  // The date picker cannot go past today.
  (function () {
    var dob = document.getElementById("dob");
    if (!dob) return;
    var t = new Date();
    dob.max = t.getFullYear() + "-" + String(t.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(t.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  })();

  function clearErrors() {
    formErr.textContent = "";
    form.querySelectorAll(".err").forEach(function (e) { e.textContent = ""; });
    form.querySelectorAll(".field").forEach(function (f) { f.classList.remove("invalid"); });
  }

  function showErrors(errors) {
    Object.keys(errors).forEach(function (key) {
      var span = form.querySelector('.err[data-for="' + key + '"]');
      if (span) {
        span.textContent = errors[key];
        span.closest(".field").classList.add("invalid");
      }
    });
    var first = form.querySelector(".field.invalid input, .field.invalid select");
    if (first) first.focus();
  }

  form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearErrors();

    var data = {};
    new FormData(form).forEach(function (v, k) { if (k !== "photo") data[k] = v; });
    data.state = stateField.hidden ? "" : (stateSel.hidden ? stateText.value : stateSel.value);
    data.photo = photoB64; // the server reports a missing photo together with any other errors

    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "Registering...";

    fetch("/api/register", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(data),
    })
      .then(function (r) {
        return r.json().then(function (body) { return { ok: r.ok, body: body }; });
      })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (res.ok) {
          var first = (res.body.fullName || "").split(" ")[0];
          document.getElementById("welcome").textContent = first
            ? "You're registered, " + first
            : "You're registered";
          document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(
            window.renderStudentCard(res.body.card, { photoSrc: photoDataUrl })
          );
          regNumberText = res.body.regNumber;
          try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, res.body.regNumber); if (res.body.token) localStorage.setItem("abecfx_token", res.body.token); } catch (e) {}
          document.getElementById("returning").hidden = true;
          document.getElementById("formCard").hidden = true;
          document.getElementById("successCard").hidden = false;
          window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
        } else if (res.body.errors) {
          showErrors(res.body.errors);
        } else {
          formErr.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        }
      })
      .catch(function () {
        formErr.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again.";
      })
      .then(function () {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = "Register";
      });
  });

  document.getElementById("printBtn").addEventListener("click", function () { window.print(); });

  document.getElementById("copyBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var text = regNumberText;
    var b = this;
    function done() {
      b.textContent = "Copied";
      setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = "Copy number"; }, 1800);
    }
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, done);
    else done();
  });
})();
```
