```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Student Portal | ABECFX</title>
  <meta name="description" content="Your ABECFX student portal: your ID card, status and expiry date in one place." />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#5a1060" />
  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
</head>
<body>
  <div class="stage">
    <aside class="panel">
      <a class="brandbar" href="/" aria-label="ABECFX home">
        <img src="/logo-white.png" alt="" />
        <span><span class="name">ABECFX</span><span class="tag">Adebass Base Edge Consult</span></span>
      </a>

      <div class="pitch">
        <h1>Your student portal.</h1>
        <p>Your ID card, quiz and Mentorship Handbook in one place.</p>
      </div>

      <nav class="nav" aria-label="Main">
        <a href="/" aria-current="page"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="9" cy="11" r="2"/><path d="M6.5 16c.6-1.4 1.6-2 2.5-2s1.9.6 2.5 2M14 10h4M14 13h3"/></svg><span>My portal</span></a>
        <a href="/register"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="8" r="3.5"/><path d="M3.5 20c0-3.6 2.9-6 6.5-6M17 11v6M14 14h6"/></svg><span>Register</span></a>
        <a href="/status"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="M16 16l4.5 4.5"/></svg><span>Find a student</span></a>
      </nav>

      <div class="social" id="siteLinks" hidden></div>
    </aside>

    <main class="sheet">
      <div class="sheet-inner">
        <p class="live" id="loading" role="status">Opening your portal...</p>

        <!-- Not signed in on this device -->
        <section id="signin" hidden>
          <h2>Open your student portal</h2>
          <p class="lead">The student portal is for registered students. Sign in with the registration number you received and the email address you registered with.</p>
          <form id="signinForm" novalidate>
            <div class="field">
              <label for="reg">Registration number</label>
              <input id="reg" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck="false" placeholder="ABECFX/26/0001" required />
            </div>
            <div class="field">
              <label for="email">Registered email</label>
              <input id="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" spellcheck="false" placeholder="you@example.com" required />
            </div>
            <p class="formErr" id="signinErr" role="alert"></p>
            <button type="submit" id="signinBtn" class="primary">Sign in</button>
          </form>
          <p class="or">New to ABECFX?</p>
          <a class="btn ghost wide" href="/register">Register as a new student</a>
          <p class="or">Just want to check a student's ID?</p>
          <a class="btn ghost wide" href="/status">Find a student</a>
        </section>

        <!-- Signed in -->
        <section id="home" hidden>
          <!-- My portal submenu -->
          <nav class="subnav" id="subnav" aria-label="My portal">
            <a href="#/card" data-view="card" aria-current="page">My ID card</a>
            <a href="#/quiz" data-view="quiz">Quiz</a>
            <a href="#/handbook" data-view="handbook">Mentorship Handbook</a>
          </nav>

          <!-- View: ID card -->
          <div id="viewCard" class="view">
            <h2 id="welcome">Welcome back</h2>
            <p class="lead">This is your student ID card.</p>
            <div class="cardwrap" id="cardMount"></div>
            <p class="live" id="liveLine" role="status"></p>
            <div class="pass-actions">
              <button type="button" id="printBtn" class="btn ghost">Print ID card</button>
              <button type="button" id="switchBtn" class="btn ghost">Sign out</button>
            </div>

            <a class="journey" id="journeyCard" href="#/handbook" aria-label="Open the Mentorship Handbook">
              <span class="journey-top"><strong>Your learning journey</strong><span class="journey-pct" id="journeyPct">&ndash;</span></span>
              <span class="pbar" aria-hidden="true"><span class="pbar-fill" id="journeyFill"></span></span>
              <span class="journey-sub" id="journeySub">Loading your progress...</span>
            </a>
          </div>

          <!-- View: Quiz -->
          <div id="viewQuiz" class="view" hidden>
            <h2>Quiz</h2>
            <p class="lead">Test what you have learned in the Mentorship Handbook.</p>
            <section class="quizcard" id="quizCard" aria-labelledby="quizTitle">
              <h3 id="quizTitle">Quiz results</h3>
              <div id="quizBody" class="quizbody"><p class="live" role="status">Loading your results...</p></div>
              <button type="button" id="quizStartBtn" class="btn primary wide" hidden>Take the quiz here</button>
              <a id="quizLink" class="btn ghost wide" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener" hidden>Take the quiz on Telegram</a>
            </section>

            <!-- The quiz runs here, inside the portal -->
            <section class="quizrun" id="quizRun" aria-labelledby="quizRunTitle" hidden>
              <div class="quizrun-bar">
                <strong id="quizRunTitle">Edge Handbook Quiz</strong>
                <button type="button" id="quizCloseBtn" class="btn ghost">Close quiz</button>
              </div>
              <p class="live" id="quizRunMsg" role="status">Opening the quiz. The first load can take up to a minute.</p>
              <iframe id="quizFrame" title="ABECFX Edge Handbook quiz" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"></iframe>
              <p class="quizrun-foot"><a id="quizTab" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Quiz not loading? Open it in a new tab</a></p>
            </section>
          </div>

          <!-- View: Mentorship Handbook -->
          <div id="viewHandbook" class="view" hidden>
            <div id="hbMount"><p class="live" role="status">Opening the handbook...</p></div>
          </div>
        </section>

        <p class="sheet-foot">&copy; <span id="year"></span> Adebass Base Edge Consult</p>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>
  <script src="/links.js"></script>
  <script src="/qr.js"></script>
  <script src="/card.js"></script>
  <script src="/handbook.js"></script>
  <script src="/portal.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

#### `public/portal.js`

```js
(function () {
  var KEY = "abecfx_reg";
  var TKEY = "abecfx_token"; // sign-in token from the website; the portal is private without it
  var loading = document.getElementById("loading");
  var signin = document.getElementById("signin");
  var home = document.getElementById("home");
  var form = document.getElementById("signinForm");
  var input = document.getElementById("reg");
  var emailInput = document.getElementById("email");
  var err = document.getElementById("signinErr");
  var btn = document.getElementById("signinBtn");

  document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

  // The quiz opens inside the portal (a frame on the quiz service) so students never leave this page.
  var currentReg = "";
  var quizOrigin = "";
  var quizRun = document.getElementById("quizRun");
  var quizFrame = document.getElementById("quizFrame");
  var quizMsg = document.getElementById("quizRunMsg");
  var quizStartBtn = document.getElementById("quizStartBtn");

  function remember(reg, token) {
    try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, reg); if (token) localStorage.setItem(TKEY, token); } catch (e) {}
  }
  function forget() { try { localStorage.removeItem(KEY); localStorage.removeItem(TKEY); } catch (e) {} }
  function saved() { try { return localStorage.getItem(KEY) || ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }
  function token() { try { return localStorage.getItem(TKEY) || ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }

  // Every private request carries the sign-in token.
  function authFetch(url, opts) {
    var o = opts || {};
    var headers = Object.assign({}, o.headers || {}, { Authorization: "Bearer " + token() });
    return fetch(url, Object.assign({}, o, { headers: headers }));
  }

  // The sign-in has expired or is not valid: back to the sign-in form.
  function signedOut(message) {
    closeQuiz(true);
    window.abecHandbook.reset();
    currentReg = "";
    currentView = "";
    forget();
    showSignin(message || "Please sign in again.");
  }

  function login(reg, email) {
    return fetch("/api/portal/login", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ reg: reg, email: email }),
    }).then(function (r) {
      return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, body: b }; });
    });
  }

  function myCard() {
    return authFetch("/api/portal/me").then(function (r) {
      return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, body: b }; });
    });
  }

  function showSignin(message) {
    loading.hidden = true;
    home.hidden = true;
    signin.hidden = false;
    err.textContent = message || "";
  }

  function showHome(d) {
    loading.hidden = true;
    signin.hidden = true;
    var first = (d.fullName || "").split(" ")[0];
    document.getElementById("welcome").textContent = first ? "Welcome back, " + first : "Welcome back";
    document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(d));
    var t = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    var note = window.expiryNotice(d);
    var live = document.getElementById("liveLine");
    live.textContent = note ? note.text : "Live record, checked at " + t;
    live.className = "live" + (note ? " live-" + note.level : "");
    home.hidden = false;
    currentReg = d.regNumber;
    closeQuiz(true);
    window.abecHandbook.reset();
    currentView = "";
    route();
  }

  // ---- My portal submenu: ID card, Quiz, Mentorship Handbook (addresses #/card, #/quiz, #/handbook/03) ----
  var VIEWS = ["card", "quiz", "handbook"];
  var currentView = "";

  function parseRoute() {
    var m = /^#\/(card|quiz|handbook)(?:\/(\d{2}))?$/.exec(location.hash || "");
    return { view: m ? m[1] : "card", chapter: m && m[2] ? m[2] : "" };
  }

  function route() {
    if (home.hidden || !currentReg) return; // not signed in: the sign-in form stays
    var r = parseRoute();
    VIEWS.forEach(function (v) {
      document.getElementById("view" + v.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + v.slice(1)).hidden = v !== r.view;
    });
    var links = document.querySelectorAll("#subnav a");
    for (var i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
      if (links[i].getAttribute("data-view") === r.view) links[i].setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
      else links[i].removeAttribute("aria-current");
    }
    document.querySelector(".sheet-inner").classList.toggle("is-wide", r.view === "handbook");
    if (r.view === "card" && currentView !== "card") window.abecHandbook.summary();
    if (r.view === "quiz" && currentView !== "quiz") showQuiz(currentReg);
    if (r.view === "handbook") window.abecHandbook.show(r.chapter);
    currentView = r.view;
    if (r.view !== "handbook" || !r.chapter) window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", route);
  window.abecHandbook.setup({ authFetch: function (u, o) { return authFetch(u, o); }, signedOut: function (m) { signedOut(m); } });

  // ---- Quiz results (taken in the ABECFX Telegram quiz bot) ----
  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }

  function showQuiz(reg) {
    var body = document.getElementById("quizBody");
    var link = document.getElementById("quizLink");
    body.replaceChildren(el("p", "live", "Loading your results..."));
    fetch("/api/config").then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(function (c) {
      if (c.quizUrl && /^https:\/\//.test(c.quizUrl)) {
        link.href = c.quizUrl;
        link.hidden = false;
        // When the quiz can run inside the portal, Telegram becomes the second choice.
        if (c.quizEmbed) link.textContent = "Take it on Telegram instead";
      }
      quizStartBtn.hidden = !c.quizEmbed || !quizRun.hidden;
    }).catch(function () {});
    authFetch("/api/results")
      .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (b) { if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(); throw new Error("signed out"); } if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error); return b; }); })
      .then(function (d) {
        body.replaceChildren();
        if (!d.latest) {
          body.appendChild(el("p", "quiz-empty", "You have not taken the quiz yet. Take it from this page and your result will appear here."));
          return;
        }
        var l = d.latest;
        var top = el("div", "quiz-top");
        top.appendChild(el("span", "grade-big g-" + l.grade, l.grade));
        var txt = el("div", "quiz-txt");
        txt.appendChild(el("strong", null, l.pct + "%  " + l.label));
        txt.appendChild(el("span", null, l.score + " of " + l.total + " correct"));
        txt.appendChild(el("span", "quiz-date", "Taken " + l.date + (d.attempts.length > 1 ? "  -  attempt " + l.attempt : "")));
        top.appendChild(txt);
        body.appendChild(top);
        if (d.best && d.best.attempt !== l.attempt) {
          body.appendChild(el("p", "quiz-best", "Best so far: " + d.best.pct + "% (grade " + d.best.grade + ")"));
        }
        if (d.attempts.length > 1) {
          var hist = el("ul", "quiz-hist");
          d.attempts.slice(0, 6).forEach(function (a) {
            var li = el("li");
            li.appendChild(el("span", null, a.date));
            li.appendChild(el("span", null, a.score + "/" + a.total + "  (" + a.pct + "%)"));
            li.appendChild(el("span", "grade g-" + a.grade, a.grade));
            hist.appendChild(li);
          });
          body.appendChild(hist);
        }
        var sc = el("p", "quiz-scale", "Grading: " + d.scale.map(function (s, i) {
          var hi = i === 0 ? 100 : d.scale[i - 1].min - 1;
          return s.grade + " " + s.min + "-" + hi;
        }).join("  |  "));
        body.appendChild(sc);
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        if (e && e.message === "signed out") return;
        body.replaceChildren(el("p", "quiz-empty", "We could not load your quiz results just now. Refresh to try again."));
      });
  }

  // ---- Take the quiz inside the portal ----
  function closeQuiz(silent) {
    if (!silent && !window.confirm("Close the quiz? A quiz you have not finished is not saved.")) return;
    quizFrame.src = "about:blank";
    quizRun.hidden = true;
    quizOrigin = "";
    quizStartBtn.hidden = true; // showQuiz decides again whether to offer it
    if (!silent && currentReg) showQuiz(currentReg);
  }

  function openQuiz() {
    if (!currentReg) return;
    quizStartBtn.disabled = true;
    authFetch("/api/quiz/session")
      .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (b) { if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(); throw new Error(""); } return { ok: r.ok, body: b }; }); })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok || !res.body.url) throw new Error(res.body.error || "We could not open the quiz just now.");
        quizOrigin = new URL(res.body.url).origin;
        quizMsg.textContent = "Opening the quiz. The first load can take up to a minute.";
        quizMsg.hidden = false;
        document.getElementById("quizTab").href = res.body.url;
        quizFrame.src = res.body.url + "&o=" + encodeURIComponent(window.location.origin);
        quizRun.hidden = false;
        quizStartBtn.hidden = true;
        quizRun.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        if (!e.message) return; // signed out: the sign-in form is already showing
        quizStartBtn.hidden = false;
        window.alert(e.message || "We could not open the quiz just now.");
      })
      .then(function () { quizStartBtn.disabled = false; });
  }

  quizStartBtn.addEventListener("click", openQuiz);
  document.getElementById("quizCloseBtn").addEventListener("click", function () { closeQuiz(false); });
  quizFrame.addEventListener("load", function () {
    if (quizFrame.getAttribute("src") && quizFrame.getAttribute("src") !== "about:blank") quizMsg.hidden = true;
  });
  // The quiz page tells the portal when a result has been saved, so the results card refreshes by itself.
  window.addEventListener("message", function (e) {
    if (!quizOrigin || e.origin !== quizOrigin || e.source !== quizFrame.contentWindow) return;
    if (e.data && e.data.type === "abecfx_quiz_saved" && currentReg) showQuiz(currentReg);
  });

  form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    err.textContent = "";
    var reg = input.value.trim();
    var email = emailInput.value.trim();
    if (!reg) { err.textContent = "Enter your registration number."; input.focus(); return; }
    if (!email) { err.textContent = "Enter the email address you registered with."; emailInput.focus(); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "Signing in...";
    login(reg, email)
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok) { err.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again."; return; }
        remember(res.body.card.regNumber, res.body.token);
        emailInput.value = "";
        showHome(res.body.card);
      })
      .catch(function () { err.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."; })
      .then(function () { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = "Sign in"; });
  });

  document.getElementById("printBtn").addEventListener("click", function () { window.print(); });
  document.getElementById("switchBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
    closeQuiz(true);
    window.abecHandbook.reset();
    currentReg = "";
    currentView = "";
    if (location.hash) history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname + location.search);
    forget();
    input.value = "";
    emailInput.value = "";
    showSignin("");
    input.focus();
  });

  // Returning student on this device: go straight to their portal if their sign-in is still valid.
  var reg = saved();
  if (token()) {
    myCard()
      .then(function (res) {
        if (res.ok) { showHome(res.body); return; }
        if (res.status === 401) { forget(); showSignin("Please sign in again."); input.value = reg; return; }
        showSignin("We could not open your portal just now. Please try again.");
        input.value = reg;
      })
      .catch(function () {
        showSignin("Network problem. Please check your connection and try again.");
        input.value = reg;
      });
  } else {
    showSignin("");
    if (reg) input.value = reg; // an earlier visit without a sign-in: ask for the email now
  }
})();
```

#### `public/handbook.js`

```js
// Mentorship Handbook inside the student portal: chapter list, reader and learning-progress bar.
// Progress = chapters marked as read / total chapters, saved on the website (Google Sheet) for the
// signed-in student. portal.js calls setup() once with its sign-in helpers.
(function () {
  var api = null; // { authFetch(url, opts), signedOut(msg) } from portal.js
  var data = null; // { chapters, final, progress }
  var openId = null; // chapter being read, or null for the chapter list
  var mount = null;
  var busy = false;

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }

  function readJson(r) {
    return r.json().then(function (b) {
      if (r.status === 401) { api.signedOut(); throw new Error("signed out"); }
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
      return b;
    });
  }

  // ---- Progress bar (also used by the small summary on the ID card page) ----
  function bar(percent) {
    var track = el("div", "pbar");
    track.setAttribute("role", "progressbar");
    track.setAttribute("aria-valuemin", "0");
    track.setAttribute("aria-valuemax", "100");
    track.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(percent));
    track.setAttribute("aria-label", "Learning progress");
    var fill = el("span", "pbar-fill");
    fill.style.width = percent + "%";
    track.appendChild(fill);
    return track;
  }

  function sub(p) {
    if (p.count === 0) return "You have not started yet. Open the handbook and read your first chapter.";
    if (p.count === p.total) return "You have read the whole handbook. Well done.";
    return p.count + " of " + p.total + " chapters read";
  }

  // Small summary on the ID card page.
  function renderJourney(p) {
    document.getElementById("journeyPct").textContent = p.percent + "%";
    document.getElementById("journeyFill").style.width = p.percent + "%";
    document.getElementById("journeySub").textContent = sub(p);
  }

  function loadSummary() {
    document.getElementById("journeySub").textContent = "Loading your progress...";
    return api.authFetch("/api/handbook/progress").then(readJson).then(renderJourney).catch(function (e) {
      if (e.message === "signed out") return;
      document.getElementById("journeyPct").textContent = "";
      document.getElementById("journeyFill").style.width = "0%";
      document.getElementById("journeySub").textContent = "Open the Mentorship Handbook to see your progress.";
    });
  }

  // ---- Chapter content ----
  function renderBlock(b) {
    var node, i;
    switch (b.t) {
      case "p": return el("p", "hb-p", b.x);
      case "h": return el("h4", "hb-h", b.x);
      case "quote": return el("blockquote", "hb-quote", b.x);
      case "note": return el("p", "hb-note", b.x);
      case "ul":
      case "ol":
        node = el(b.t, "hb-list");
        for (i = 0; i < b.items.length; i++) node.appendChild(el("li", null, b.items[i]));
        return node;
      case "cards":
        node = el("div", "hb-cards");
        for (i = 0; i < b.items.length; i++) {
          var c = el("div", "hb-card");
          c.appendChild(el("strong", null, b.items[i].h));
          c.appendChild(el("span", null, b.items[i].x));
          node.appendChild(c);
        }
        return node;
      case "table":
        var wrap = el("div", "hb-tablewrap");
        var t = el("table", "hb-table");
        var thead = el("thead"), hr = el("tr");
        b.head.forEach(function (h) { hr.appendChild(el("th", null, h)); });
        thead.appendChild(hr);
        t.appendChild(thead);
        var tb = el("tbody");
        b.rows.forEach(function (r) {
          var tr = el("tr");
          r.forEach(function (cell) { tr.appendChild(el("td", null, cell)); });
          tb.appendChild(tr);
        });
        t.appendChild(tb);
        wrap.appendChild(t);
        return wrap;
    }
    return el("span");
  }

  function isRead(id) { return data.progress.read.indexOf(id) !== -1; }

  function openPdf(btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    api.authFetch("/api/handbook/pdf-link").then(readJson).then(function (b) {
      window.open(b.url, "_blank", "noopener");
    }).catch(function (e) {
      if (e.message !== "signed out") window.alert(e.message);
    }).then(function () { btn.disabled = false; });
  }

  function pdfButton(label) {
    var b = el("button", "btn ghost wide", label);
    b.type = "button";
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { openPdf(b); });
    return b;
  }

  // ---- Screens ----
  function header(p) {
    var box = el("section", "hb-progress");
    var top = el("div", "hb-progress-top");
    top.appendChild(el("strong", null, "Your learning journey"));
    top.appendChild(el("span", "hb-pct", p.percent + "%"));
    box.appendChild(top);
    box.appendChild(bar(p.percent));
    box.appendChild(el("p", "hb-progress-sub", sub(p)));
    return box;
  }

  function hasVideo(id) { return !!(data.videos && data.videos[id]); }

  // The video tutorial for a chapter (chapters 01-12). Until the editor adds one it says "coming soon".
  function videoBlock(id) {
    if (id === "00") return null;
    var box = el("section", "hb-video");
    box.appendChild(el("h3", null, "Video tutorial"));
    var vid = data.videos && data.videos[id];
    if (!vid || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(vid)) {
      box.appendChild(el("p", "hb-video-soon", "The video for this chapter is coming soon. Check back later."));
      return box;
    }
    var frame = el("div", "hb-video-frame");
    var f = document.createElement("iframe");
    f.src = "https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/" + vid + "?rel=0";
    f.title = "Video tutorial";
    f.loading = "lazy";
    f.allow = "accelerometer; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; fullscreen";
    f.setAttribute("allowfullscreen", "");
    f.setAttribute("referrerpolicy", "strict-origin-when-cross-origin");
    frame.appendChild(f);
    box.appendChild(frame);
    var a = el("a", "hb-video-link", "Not playing? Watch on YouTube");
    a.href = "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=" + vid;
    a.target = "_blank";
    a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    box.appendChild(a);
    return box;
  }

  function renderList() {
    var p = data.progress;
    mount.replaceChildren();
    mount.appendChild(el("h2", null, "Mentorship Handbook"));
    mount.appendChild(el("p", "lead", "The Edge Handbook your quiz comes from. Read each chapter, then mark it as read to move your progress bar."));
    mount.appendChild(header(p));

    var list = el("ol", "hb-chapters");
    data.chapters.forEach(function (c) {
      var li = el("li");
      var a = el("a", "hb-chapter" + (isRead(c.id) ? " is-read" : ""));
      a.href = "#/handbook/" + c.id;
      a.appendChild(el("span", "hb-num", isRead(c.id) ? "✓" : c.id));
      var txt = el("span", "hb-chapter-txt");
      txt.appendChild(el("strong", null, c.title));
      txt.appendChild(el("span", null, (isRead(c.id) ? "Read" : "Not read yet") + (hasVideo(c.id) ? " · Video tutorial" : "")));
      a.appendChild(txt);
      li.appendChild(a);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    mount.appendChild(list);

    if (p.count === p.total) {
      var fin = el("section", "hb-final");
      fin.appendChild(el("h3", null, data.final.title));
      data.final.paragraphs.forEach(function (t) { fin.appendChild(el("p", null, t)); });
      var q = el("a", "btn primary wide", "Take the quiz");
      q.href = "#/quiz";
      fin.appendChild(q);
      mount.appendChild(fin);
    }
    var foot = el("div", "hb-foot");
    foot.appendChild(el("p", "hb-foot-note", "The PDF also has the charts and the practice workbook pages."));
    foot.appendChild(pdfButton("Open the full handbook PDF"));
    mount.appendChild(foot);
  }

  function renderChapter(id, keepScroll) {
    var idx = -1;
    data.chapters.forEach(function (c, i) { if (c.id === id) idx = i; });
    if (idx < 0) { location.hash = "#/handbook"; return; }
    var c = data.chapters[idx];
    var p = data.progress;
    mount.replaceChildren();

    var back = el("a", "hb-back", "← All chapters");
    back.href = "#/handbook";
    mount.appendChild(back);

    var mini = el("div", "hb-mini");
    mini.appendChild(el("span", null, "Progress " + p.percent + "%"));
    mini.appendChild(bar(p.percent));
    mount.appendChild(mini);

    var art = el("article", "hb-article");
    art.appendChild(el("p", "hb-kicker", c.id === "00" ? "Introduction" : "Chapter " + c.id));
    art.appendChild(el("h2", null, c.title));
    art.appendChild(el("p", "hb-tagline", c.tagline));
    var vb = videoBlock(c.id);
    if (vb) art.appendChild(vb);
    c.blocks.forEach(function (b) { art.appendChild(renderBlock(b)); });
    mount.appendChild(art);

    var done = isRead(c.id);
    var markBtn = el("button", (done ? "btn ghost wide" : "btn primary wide") + " hb-mark", done ? "✓ Read. Mark as not read" : "Mark this chapter as read");
    markBtn.type = "button";
    var msg = el("p", "hb-msg");
    msg.setAttribute("role", "status");
    markBtn.addEventListener("click", function () { mark(c.id, !isRead(c.id), markBtn, msg); });
    mount.appendChild(markBtn);
    mount.appendChild(msg);

    var nav = el("div", "hb-pager");
    if (idx > 0) {
      var prev = el("a", "btn ghost", "← Previous");
      prev.href = "#/handbook/" + data.chapters[idx - 1].id;
      nav.appendChild(prev);
    } else nav.appendChild(el("span"));
    if (idx < data.chapters.length - 1) {
      var next = el("a", "btn ghost", "Next →");
      next.href = "#/handbook/" + data.chapters[idx + 1].id;
      nav.appendChild(next);
    }
    mount.appendChild(nav);
    mount.appendChild(pdfButton("Open the full handbook PDF"));
    if (!keepScroll) window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }

  function render() {
    if (!data) return;
    if (openId) renderChapter(openId); else renderList();
  }

  // Save one chapter. The bar moves right away and goes back if the save fails.
  function mark(id, read, btn, msg) {
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    btn.disabled = true;
    msg.textContent = "Saving...";
    api.authFetch("/api/handbook/progress", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ chapter: id, read: read }),
    }).then(readJson).then(function (p) {
      data.progress = p;
      renderJourney(p);
      busy = false;
      renderChapter(id, true);
      var again = document.querySelector("#hbMount .hb-mark");
      if (again) again.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      var m = document.querySelector("#hbMount .hb-msg");
      if (m) {
        m.textContent = read
          ? (p.count === p.total ? "Saved. You have read every chapter: 100% complete." : "Saved. Your progress is now " + p.percent + "%.")
          : "Saved. Your progress is now " + p.percent + "%.";
      }
    }).catch(function (e) {
      busy = false;
      if (e.message === "signed out") return;
      btn.disabled = false;
      msg.textContent = e.message || "We could not save that. Please try again.";
    });
  }

  function load() {
    mount.replaceChildren(el("p", "live", "Opening the handbook..."));
    return api.authFetch("/api/handbook").then(readJson).then(function (d) {
      data = d;
      renderJourney(d.progress);
      render();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      if (e.message === "signed out") return;
      var box = el("div");
      box.appendChild(el("p", "quiz-empty", e.message || "We could not open the handbook just now."));
      var retry = el("button", "btn ghost", "Try again");
      retry.type = "button";
      retry.addEventListener("click", load);
      box.appendChild(retry);
      mount.replaceChildren(box);
    });
  }

  window.abecHandbook = {
    setup: function (a) { api = a; mount = document.getElementById("hbMount"); },
    // Called by the portal each time the Handbook view is shown. id is a chapter id or "".
    show: function (id) {
      openId = id || null;
      if (data) render(); else load();
    },
    summary: function () { return loadSummary(); },
    reset: function () { data = null; openId = null; busy = false; if (mount) mount.replaceChildren(); },
  };
})();
```

#### `public/status.html`

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Student ID | ABECFX</title>
  <meta name="description" content="Find an ABECFX student ID card and check a student's live status using their registration number." />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#5a1060" />
  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
</head>
<body>
  <div class="stage">
    <aside class="panel">
      <a class="brandbar" href="/" aria-label="ABECFX home">
        <img src="/logo-white.png" alt="" />
        <span><span class="name">ABECFX</span><span class="tag">Adebass Base Edge Consult</span></span>
      </a>

      <div class="pitch">
        <h1>Find a student ID card.</h1>
        <p>Enter a registration number, or scan the QR code on an ID card, to see a student's live status.</p>
      </div>

      <nav class="nav" aria-label="Main">
        <a href="/"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="9" cy="11" r="2"/><path d="M6.5 16c.6-1.4 1.6-2 2.5-2s1.9.6 2.5 2M14 10h4M14 13h3"/></svg><span>My portal</span></a>
        <a href="/register"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="8" r="3.5"/><path d="M3.5 20c0-3.6 2.9-6 6.5-6M17 11v6M14 14h6"/></svg><span>Register</span></a>
        <a href="/status" aria-current="page"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="M16 16l4.5 4.5"/></svg><span>Find a student</span></a>
      </nav>

      <div class="social" id="siteLinks" hidden></div>
    </aside>

    <main class="sheet">
      <div class="sheet-inner">
        <h2>Student ID card</h2>
        <p class="lead">Enter the registration number, for example ABECFX/26/0001.</p>

        <form id="lookupForm" novalidate>
          <div class="search">
            <div class="field">
              <label for="reg" class="sr-only">Registration number</label>
              <input id="reg" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck="false" placeholder="ABECFX/26/0001" required />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" id="lookupBtn" class="primary" style="width:auto">Find ID card</button>
          </div>
          <p class="formErr" id="lookupErr" role="alert" style="margin-top:12px"></p>
        </form>

        <div class="result" id="result" hidden>
          <div class="cardwrap" id="cardMount"></div>
          <p class="live" id="liveLine" role="status"></p>
          <div class="pass-actions">
            <button type="button" id="printBtn" class="btn ghost">Print ID card</button>
            <button type="button" id="linkBtn" class="btn ghost">Copy link</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <p class="sheet-foot">&copy; <span id="year"></span> Adebass Base Edge Consult</p>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>
  <script src="/links.js"></script>
  <script src="/qr.js"></script>
  <script src="/card.js"></script>
  <script src="/status.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

#### `public/status.js`

```js
(function () {
  var form = document.getElementById("lookupForm");
  var input = document.getElementById("reg");
  var err = document.getElementById("lookupErr");
  var btn = document.getElementById("lookupBtn");
  var result = document.getElementById("result");
  var mount = document.getElementById("cardMount");
  var liveLine = document.getElementById("liveLine");
  var currentReg = "";

  document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

  function show(d) {
    currentReg = d.regNumber;
    mount.replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(d));
    var t = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    var note = window.expiryNotice(d);
    liveLine.textContent = note ? note.text : "Live record, checked at " + t;
    liveLine.className = "live" + (note ? " live-" + note.level : "");
    result.hidden = false;
    try { history.replaceState(null, "", "/status?reg=" + d.regNumber); } catch (e) {}
    if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 900px)").matches) {
      result.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    }
  }

  function search() {
    err.textContent = "";
    result.hidden = true;
    var reg = input.value.trim();
    if (!reg) {
      err.textContent = "Enter a registration number to search.";
      input.focus();
      return;
    }
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "Searching...";
    fetch("/api/lookup?reg=" + encodeURIComponent(reg))
      .then(function (r) {
        return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, body: b }; });
      })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok) {
          err.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
          return;
        }
        show(res.body);
      })
      .catch(function () {
        err.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again.";
      })
      .then(function () {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = "Find ID card";
      });
  }

  form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    search();
  });

  document.getElementById("printBtn").addEventListener("click", function () { window.print(); });
  document.getElementById("linkBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var b = this;
    var link = location.origin + "/status?reg=" + currentReg;
    function done() {
      b.textContent = "Link copied";
      setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = "Copy link"; }, 1800);
    }
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).then(done, done);
    else done();
  });

  // Opened from a QR code (or shared link): look the student up straight away.
  var pre = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("reg");
  if (pre) {
    input.value = pre;
    search();
  }
})();
```

#### `public/card.js`

```js
/*
 * Student ID card renderer (shared by the registration success screen and the status page).
 * Needs qr.js loaded first. All text is set with textContent, never innerHTML.
 */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var SVG_NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  var stampCounter = 0;

  var STATUS_CLASS = {
    "Uncertified": "s-uncertified",
    "Certified": "s-certified",
    "Graduate": "s-graduate",
    "Alumni Member": "s-alumni",
    "Mentor": "s-mentor",
    "Staff Member": "s-staff",
    "CEO": "s-ceo"
  };

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }
  function svgEl(tag, attrs) {
    var e = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
    Object.keys(attrs || {}).forEach(function (k) { e.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); });
    return e;
  }
  function initials(name) {
    var parts = String(name || "").trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (!parts.length) return "?";
    var a = parts[0].charAt(0), b = parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 1].charAt(0) : "";
    return (a + b).toUpperCase();
  }
  function emojiFlag(code) {
    if (!/^[a-z]{2}$/i.test(code || "")) return "";
    return String.fromCodePoint.apply(null, code.toUpperCase().split("").map(function (c) {
      return 0x1f1e6 + c.charCodeAt(0) - 65;
    }));
  }

  // Round stamp: "Certified graduate of ABEC-FX" (Certified and Graduate students) or "Alumni member" (alumni).
  function buildStamp(status, dateText) {
    var isGrad = status !== "Alumni Member";
    var top = isGrad ? "CERTIFIED GRADUATE" : "ALUMNI MEMBER";
    var id = "stamp" + ++stampCounter;

    var svg = svgEl("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 200 200", "class": "idc-stamp-svg", role: "img",
      "aria-label": isGrad ? "Certified graduate of ABEC-FX" : "Alumni member of ABEC-FX" });

    var defs = svgEl("defs");
    defs.appendChild(svgEl("path", { id: id + "t", d: "M 100,100 m -67,0 a 67,67 0 1,1 134,0" }));
    defs.appendChild(svgEl("path", { id: id + "b", d: "M 100,100 m -78,0 a 78,78 0 0,0 156,0" }));
    svg.appendChild(defs);

    svg.appendChild(svgEl("circle", { cx: 100, cy: 100, r: 95, "class": "st-ring st-ring-thick" }));
    svg.appendChild(svgEl("circle", { cx: 100, cy: 100, r: 89, "class": "st-ring st-ring-thin" }));
    svg.appendChild(svgEl("circle", { cx: 100, cy: 100, r: 53, "class": "st-ring st-ring-thin" }));

    function arcText(pathId, str, size) {
      var t = svgEl("text", { "class": "st-arc", "font-size": size });
      var tp = svgEl("textPath", { href: "#" + pathId, startOffset: "50%", "text-anchor": "middle" });
      tp.setAttributeNS("http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink", "xlink:href", "#" + pathId);
      tp.textContent = str;
      t.appendChild(tp);
      return t;
    }
    svg.appendChild(arcText(id + "t", top, 13.5));
    svg.appendChild(arcText(id + "b", "ADEBASS BASE EDGE CONSULT", 10.5));

    var stars = svgEl("text", { x: 100, y: 83, "text-anchor": "middle", "class": "st-stars", "font-size": 10 });
    stars.textContent = "★ ★ ★";
    svg.appendChild(stars);

    var name = svgEl("text", { x: 100, y: 107, "text-anchor": "middle", "class": "st-name", "font-size": 20 });
    name.textContent = "ABEC-FX";
    svg.appendChild(name);

    svg.appendChild(svgEl("line", { x1: 72, y1: 115, x2: 128, y2: 115, "class": "st-line" }));

    var date = svgEl("text", { x: 100, y: 129, "text-anchor": "middle", "class": "st-date", "font-size": 9.5 });
    date.textContent = dateText ? String(dateText).toUpperCase() : "OFFICIAL";
    svg.appendChild(date);
    return svg;
  }

  // Text for the "expires" line on the card.
  function expiryLine(d) {
    if (d.expiryKind === "date") {
      if (d.expired) return { text: "Expired " + d.expires, cls: "is-expired" };
      if (d.daysLeft === 0) return { text: "Expires today", cls: "is-soon" };
      return { text: "Expires " + d.expires, cls: d.daysLeft <= 14 ? "is-soon" : "" };
    }
    if (d.expiryKind === "unknown") return { text: "Expiry date not set", cls: "" };
    return { text: "No expiry", cls: "" };
  }

  // Message shown under the card when an ID has expired or is about to.
  window.expiryNotice = function (d) {
    if (d.expiryKind !== "date") return null;
    if (d.expired) return { level: "bad", text: "This ID expired on " + d.expires + ". Speak to ABECFX to renew it." };
    if (d.daysLeft <= 14) {
      var when = d.daysLeft === 0 ? "today" : d.daysLeft === 1 ? "tomorrow" : "in " + d.daysLeft + " days";
      return { level: "soon", text: "This ID expires " + when + " (" + d.expires + ")." };
    }
    return null;
  };

  /**
   * d: card data from /api/lookup or /api/register
   * opts.photoSrc: use this image instead of /api/photo (e.g. the just-taken photo)
   */
  window.renderStudentCard = function (d, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var status = d.status || "Uncertified";
    var card = el("article", "idc");
    card.setAttribute("data-status", status.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-"));
    card.setAttribute("aria-label", "Student ID card for " + d.fullName);

    // ---- header
    var top = el("div", "idc-top");
    var brand = el("div", "idc-brand");
    var logo = el("img");
    logo.src = "/logo-white.png"; logo.alt = ""; logo.width = 30; logo.height = 23;
    brand.appendChild(logo);
    brand.appendChild(el("span", null, "ABECFX"));
    top.appendChild(brand);
    top.appendChild(el("span", "idc-kind", "Student ID card"));
    card.appendChild(top);

    // ---- passport photo + national flag + facts
    var hero = el("div", "idc-hero");
    var photoBox = el("div", "idc-photo");
    var fallback = el("span", "idc-initials", initials(d.fullName));
    fallback.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    photoBox.appendChild(fallback);
    var photo = el("img", "idc-photo-img");
    photo.alt = "Passport photograph of " + d.fullName;
    photo.addEventListener("load", function () { photoBox.classList.add("has-photo"); });
    photo.addEventListener("error", function () { photo.remove(); });
    photo.src = opts.photoSrc || "/api/photo?reg=" + encodeURIComponent(d.regNumber);
    photoBox.appendChild(photo);

    if (d.countryCode) {
      var flagWrap = el("div", "idc-flag");
      var flag = el("img");
      flag.alt = (d.country || "National") + " flag";
      flag.src = "https://flagcdn.com/w80/" + d.countryCode + ".png";
      flag.srcset = "https://flagcdn.com/w80/" + d.countryCode + ".png 1x, https://flagcdn.com/w160/" + d.countryCode + ".png 2x";
      flag.addEventListener("error", function () {
        flagWrap.textContent = emojiFlag(d.countryCode);
        flagWrap.classList.add("idc-flag-emoji");
      });
      flagWrap.appendChild(flag);
      photoBox.appendChild(flagWrap);
    }
    hero.appendChild(photoBox);

    var facts = el("dl", "idc-facts");
    function fact(label, value) {
      var row = el("div");
      row.appendChild(el("dt", null, label));
      row.appendChild(el("dd", null, value || "Not recorded"));
      facts.appendChild(row);
    }
    fact("Country", d.country);
    if (d.state) fact("State", d.state);
    fact("Gender", d.gender);
    fact("Programme", d.programme);
    fact("Admitted", d.admitted ? String(d.admitted) : "");
    hero.appendChild(facts);
    card.appendChild(hero);

    // ---- name + registration number
    if (d.expired) {
      card.classList.add("idc-is-expired");
      var mark = el("div", "idc-expired-mark", "EXPIRED");
      mark.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
      card.appendChild(mark);
    }
    card.appendChild(el("h3", "idc-name", d.fullName));
    card.appendChild(el("p", "idc-reg", d.regNumber));

    // ---- status (+ stamp for graduates and alumni)
    var row = el("div", "idc-statusrow");
    var col = el("div", "idc-statuscol");
    var chip = el("span", "status " + (STATUS_CLASS[status] || "s-uncertified"));
    chip.appendChild(el("span", null, status));
    col.appendChild(chip);
    col.appendChild(el("span", "idc-since",
      status === "Uncertified" ? "Awaiting certification"
        : "Since " + (d.statusDate || "date not recorded")));
    row.appendChild(col);
    if (status === "Graduate" || status === "Certified" || status === "Alumni Member") {
      var stampWrap = el("div", "idc-stamp");
      stampWrap.appendChild(buildStamp(status, d.statusDate));
      row.appendChild(stampWrap);
    }
    card.appendChild(row);

    // ---- QR code: opens this student's live status
    var url = (opts.origin || location.origin) + "/status?reg=" + d.regNumber;
    var verify = el("div", "idc-verify");
    var qr = el("div", "idc-qr");
    try { qr.appendChild(window.QR.toSvg(url, { border: 1 })); } catch (e) { qr.textContent = ""; }
    verify.appendChild(qr);
    var vt = el("div", "idc-vtext");
    vt.appendChild(el("strong", null, "Scan to check status"));
    vt.appendChild(el("span", null, "Opens this student's live record."));
    if (d.issued) vt.appendChild(el("span", "idc-issued", "Issued " + d.issued));
    var exp = expiryLine(d);
    vt.appendChild(el("span", "idc-expiry " + exp.cls, exp.text));
    verify.appendChild(vt);
    card.appendChild(verify);

    var foot = el("div", "idc-foot");
    foot.appendChild(el("span", null, "Adebass Base Edge Consult"));
    card.appendChild(foot);
    return card;
  };
})();
```

#### `public/qr.js`

```js
/*
 * Minimal QR Code generator (byte mode, error correction level M, versions 1-10).
 * Written for the ABECFX ID card so the site needs no outside QR service.
 * Exposes: QR.encode(text) -> { size, modules[y][x] }  and  QR.toSvg(text, { border })
 */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var EC_PER_BLOCK = [0, 10, 16, 26, 18, 24, 16, 18, 22, 22, 26]; // level M
  var NUM_BLOCKS = [0, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 4, 4, 4, 5, 5];             // level M
  var MAX_VERSION = 10;

  function rawModules(ver) {
    var r = (16 * ver + 128) * ver + 64;
    if (ver >= 2) {
      var n = Math.floor(ver / 7) + 2;
      r -= (25 * n - 10) * n - 55;
      if (ver >= 7) r -= 36;
    }
    return r;
  }
  function dataCodewords(ver) {
    return Math.floor(rawModules(ver) / 8) - EC_PER_BLOCK[ver] * NUM_BLOCKS[ver];
  }

  // ---- Reed-Solomon over GF(256), polynomial 0x11D ----
  function gfMul(x, y) {
    var z = 0;
    for (var i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
      z = (z << 1) ^ ((z >>> 7) * 0x11d);
      z ^= ((y >>> i) & 1) * x;
    }
    return z;
  }
  function rsDivisor(degree) {
    var result = [], i, j;
    for (i = 0; i < degree - 1; i++) result.push(0);
    result.push(1);
    var root = 1;
    for (i = 0; i < degree; i++) {
      for (j = 0; j < result.length; j++) {
        result[j] = gfMul(result[j], root);
        if (j + 1 < result.length) result[j] ^= result[j + 1];
      }
      root = gfMul(root, 2);
    }
    return result;
  }
  function rsRemainder(data, divisor) {
    var result = divisor.map(function () { return 0; });
    data.forEach(function (b) {
      var factor = b ^ result.shift();
      result.push(0);
      divisor.forEach(function (coef, i) { result[i] ^= gfMul(coef, factor); });
    });
    return result;
  }

  function addEccAndInterleave(data, ver) {
    var numBlocks = NUM_BLOCKS[ver];
    var eccLen = EC_PER_BLOCK[ver];
    var raw = Math.floor(rawModules(ver) / 8);
    var numShort = numBlocks - (raw % numBlocks);
    var shortLen = Math.floor(raw / numBlocks);
    var blocks = [];
    var div = rsDivisor(eccLen);
    for (var i = 0, k = 0; i < numBlocks; i++) {
      var dat = data.slice(k, k + shortLen - eccLen + (i < numShort ? 0 : 1));
      k += dat.length;
      var ecc = rsRemainder(dat, div);
      if (i < numShort) dat.push(0);
      blocks.push(dat.concat(ecc));
    }
    var result = [];
    for (var c = 0; c < blocks[0].length; c++) {
      blocks.forEach(function (block, j) {
        if (c !== shortLen - eccLen || j >= numShort) result.push(block[c]);
      });
    }
    return result;
  }

  function utf8Bytes(str) {
    var s = unescape(encodeURIComponent(str)), out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) out.push(s.charCodeAt(i));
    return out;
  }
  function getBit(x, i) { return ((x >>> i) & 1) !== 0; }

  function encode(text) {
    var bytes = utf8Bytes(text);
    var ver, capBits;
    for (ver = 1; ver <= MAX_VERSION; ver++) {
      capBits = dataCodewords(ver) * 8;
      var ccBits = ver < 10 ? 8 : 16;
      if (4 + ccBits + bytes.length * 8 <= capBits) break;
    }
    if (ver > MAX_VERSION) throw new Error("QR text too long");

    var bits = [];
    function push(val, len) { for (var i = len - 1; i >= 0; i--) bits.push((val >>> i) & 1); }
    push(4, 4);
    push(bytes.length, ver < 10 ? 8 : 16);
    bytes.forEach(function (b) { push(b, 8); });
    push(0, Math.min(4, capBits - bits.length));
    push(0, (8 - (bits.length % 8)) % 8);
    for (var pad = 0xec; bits.length < capBits; pad ^= 0xec ^ 0x11) push(pad, 8);

    var data = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < bits.length; i += 8) {
      var v = 0;
      for (var j = 0; j < 8; j++) v = (v << 1) | bits[i + j];
      data.push(v);
    }
    var all = addEccAndInterleave(data, ver);

    var size = ver * 4 + 17;
    var modules = [], isFn = [];
    for (var y = 0; y < size; y++) {
      modules.push(new Array(size).fill(false));
      isFn.push(new Array(size).fill(false));
    }
    function setFn(x, y, dark) { modules[y][x] = dark; isFn[y][x] = true; }

    function drawFinder(cx, cy) {
      for (var dy = -4; dy <= 4; dy++) {
        for (var dx = -4; dx <= 4; dx++) {
          var dist = Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy));
          var xx = cx + dx, yy = cy + dy;
          if (xx >= 0 && xx < size && yy >= 0 && yy < size) setFn(xx, yy, dist !== 2 && dist !== 4);
        }
      }
    }
    function drawAlign(cx, cy) {
      for (var dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++)
        for (var dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++)
          setFn(cx + dx, cy + dy, Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) !== 1);
    }
    function alignPositions() {
      if (ver === 1) return [];
      var n = Math.floor(ver / 7) + 2;
      var step = Math.ceil((ver * 4 + 4) / (n * 2 - 2)) * 2;
      var res = [6];
      for (var pos = size - 7; res.length < n; pos -= step) res.splice(1, 0, pos);
      return res;
    }
    function drawFormat(mask) {
      var d = (0 << 3) | mask; // level M = 00
      var rem = d, i;
      for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) rem = (rem << 1) ^ ((rem >>> 9) * 0x537);
      var b = ((d << 10) | rem) ^ 0x5412;
      for (i = 0; i <= 5; i++) setFn(8, i, getBit(b, i));
      setFn(8, 7, getBit(b, 6));
      setFn(8, 8, getBit(b, 7));
      setFn(7, 8, getBit(b, 8));
      for (i = 9; i < 15; i++) setFn(14 - i, 8, getBit(b, i));
      for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) setFn(size - 1 - i, 8, getBit(b, i));
      for (i = 8; i < 15; i++) setFn(8, size - 15 + i, getBit(b, i));
      setFn(8, size - 8, true);
    }
    function drawVersion() {
      if (ver < 7) return;
      var rem = ver, i;
      for (i = 0; i < 12; i++) rem = (rem << 1) ^ ((rem >>> 11) * 0x1f25);
      var b = (ver << 12) | rem;
      for (i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
        var bit = getBit(b, i), a = size - 11 + (i % 3), c = Math.floor(i / 3);
        setFn(a, c, bit);
        setFn(c, a, bit);
      }
    }

    for (i = 0; i < size; i++) { setFn(6, i, i % 2 === 0); setFn(i, 6, i % 2 === 0); }
    drawFinder(3, 3); drawFinder(size - 4, 3); drawFinder(3, size - 4);
    var ap = alignPositions(), last = ap.length - 1;
    for (i = 0; i < ap.length; i++) {
      for (j = 0; j < ap.length; j++) {
        if ((i === 0 && j === 0) || (i === 0 && j === last) || (i === last && j === 0)) continue;
        drawAlign(ap[i], ap[j]);
      }
    }
    var j;
    drawFormat(0);
    drawVersion();

    // place codewords in the zig-zag order
    var n = 0;
    for (var right = size - 1; right >= 1; right -= 2) {
      if (right === 6) right = 5;
      for (var vert = 0; vert < size; vert++) {
        for (j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
          var x = right - j;
          var upward = ((right + 1) & 2) === 0;
          var yy = upward ? size - 1 - vert : vert;
          if (!isFn[yy][x] && n < all.length * 8) {
            modules[yy][x] = getBit(all[n >>> 3], 7 - (n & 7));
            n++;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    function applyMask(mask) {
      for (var y = 0; y < size; y++) {
        for (var x = 0; x < size; x++) {
          var inv;
          switch (mask) {
            case 0: inv = (x + y) % 2 === 0; break;
            case 1: inv = y % 2 === 0; break;
            case 2: inv = x % 3 === 0; break;
            case 3: inv = (x + y) % 3 === 0; break;
            case 4: inv = (Math.floor(x / 3) + Math.floor(y / 2)) % 2 === 0; break;
            case 5: inv = ((x * y) % 2) + ((x * y) % 3) === 0; break;
            case 6: inv = (((x * y) % 2) + ((x * y) % 3)) % 2 === 0; break;
            default: inv = (((x + y) % 2) + ((x * y) % 3)) % 2 === 0;
          }
          if (!isFn[y][x] && inv) modules[y][x] = !modules[y][x];
        }
      }
    }

    function penalty() {
      var score = 0, x, y, run, k, line;
      var dark = 0;
      for (y = 0; y < size; y++) for (x = 0; x < size; x++) if (modules[y][x]) dark++;
      function runs(get) {
        for (var a = 0; a < size; a++) {
          run = 1;
          for (var b = 1; b <= size; b++) {
            if (b < size && get(a, b) === get(a, b - 1)) run++;
            else { if (run >= 5) score += 3 + (run - 5); run = 1; }
          }
        }
      }
      runs(function (a, b) { return modules[a][b]; });
      runs(function (a, b) { return modules[b][a]; });
      for (y = 0; y < size - 1; y++)
        for (x = 0; x < size - 1; x++) {
          var c = modules[y][x];
          if (c === modules[y][x + 1] && c === modules[y + 1][x] && c === modules[y + 1][x + 1]) score += 3;
        }
      function finderLike(s) {
        var count = 0, idx = -1, pats = ["00001011101", "10111010000"];
        pats.forEach(function (p) {
          idx = s.indexOf(p);
          while (idx !== -1) { count++; idx = s.indexOf(p, idx + 1); }
        });
        return count;
      }
      for (y = 0; y < size; y++) {
        line = ""; for (x = 0; x < size; x++) line += modules[y][x] ? "1" : "0";
        score += 40 * finderLike(line);
      }
      for (x = 0; x < size; x++) {
        line = ""; for (y = 0; y < size; y++) line += modules[y][x] ? "1" : "0";
        score += 40 * finderLike(line);
      }
      k = Math.ceil(Math.abs(dark * 20 - size * size * 10) / (size * size)) - 1;
      score += Math.max(0, k) * 10;
      return score;
    }

    var best = 0, bestScore = Infinity, m;
    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) {
      applyMask(m);
      drawFormat(m);
      var p = penalty();
      if (p < bestScore) { bestScore = p; best = m; }
      applyMask(m); // undo
    }
    applyMask(best);
    drawFormat(best);
    return { size: size, version: ver, mask: best, modules: modules };
  }

  function toSvg(text, opts) {
    var border = opts && opts.border != null ? opts.border : 2;
    var qr = encode(text);
    var ns = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
    var d = "";
    for (var y = 0; y < qr.size; y++)
      for (var x = 0; x < qr.size; x++)
        if (qr.modules[y][x]) d += "M" + (x + border) + "," + (y + border) + "h1v1h-1z";
    var total = qr.size + border * 2;
    var svg = document.createElementNS(ns, "svg");
    svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 " + total + " " + total);
    svg.setAttribute("shape-rendering", "crispEdges");
    svg.setAttribute("role", "img");
    svg.setAttribute("aria-label", "QR code to check this student's status");
    var bg = document.createElementNS(ns, "rect");
    bg.setAttribute("width", total); bg.setAttribute("height", total); bg.setAttribute("fill", "#fff");
    var path = document.createElementNS(ns, "path");
    path.setAttribute("d", d); path.setAttribute("fill", "#14081a");
    svg.appendChild(bg); svg.appendChild(path);
    return svg;
  }

  var api = { encode: encode, toSvg: toSvg };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) module.exports = api;
  else window.QR = api;
})();
```

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow" />
  <title>Editor dashboard | ABECFX</title>
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#5a1060" />
  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/admin.css" />
</head>
<body class="adm">
  <header class="adm-top">
    <a class="adm-brand" href="/" aria-label="ABECFX home">
      <img src="/logo-white.png" alt="" width="34" height="26" />
      <span><b>ABECFX</b><i>Editor dashboard</i></span>
    </a>
    <div class="adm-actions" id="topActions" hidden>
      <span class="adm-stamp" id="stamp"></span>
      <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="refreshBtn">Refresh</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="logoutBtn">Sign out</button>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main class="adm-main">
    <section id="login" class="adm-login">
      <h1>Editor sign in</h1>
      <p class="lead">Enter the editor password to see student statistics and quiz results.</p>
      <form id="loginForm" novalidate>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="pw">Password</label>
          <input id="pw" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required />
        </div>
        <p class="formErr" id="loginErr" role="alert"></p>
        <button type="submit" class="primary" id="loginBtn">Sign in</button>
      </form>
    </section>

    <section id="dash" hidden>
      <nav class="adm-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Dashboard sections">
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-overview" aria-selected="true" aria-controls="view-overview">Statistics</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-quiz" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-quiz">Quiz results</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-links" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-links">Site links</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-videos" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-videos">Chapter videos</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-database" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-database">Database</button>
      </nav>

      <p class="adm-hint" id="statsHint">Tap or click any number, bar or chart column to see the students behind it.</p>

      <div id="view-overview" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-overview">
        <div class="kpis" id="kpis"></div>

        <div class="grid">
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Certification status</h3><div id="c-status"></div><p class="adm-note" id="c-status-note"></p></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Gender</h3><div id="c-gender"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Programme enrolled</h3><div id="c-programme"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Year joined</h3><div id="c-year"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Country of residence</h3><div id="c-country"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>State of residence</h3><div id="c-state"></div></article>
        </div>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <div class="adm-card-head">
            <h3>Month joined</h3>
            <label class="adm-select">Year <select id="yearSel"></select></label>
          </div>
          <div id="c-monthyear"></div>
        </article>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Monthly student growth</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">Bars are new students each month; the line is the running total.</p>
          <div id="c-growth"></div>
          <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="growthTable"></table></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="undatedNote"></p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-quiz" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-quiz" hidden>
        <div class="kpis" id="quizKpis"></div>
        <div class="grid">
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Grades (latest attempt per student)</h3><div id="c-grades"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Grading scale</h3><div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="c-scale"></table></div></article>
        </div>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <div class="adm-card-head">
            <h3>All results</h3>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="csvBtn">Download CSV</button>
          </div>
          <div class="filters">
            <input id="q" type="search" placeholder="Search name or reg number" aria-label="Search results" />
            <select id="gradeFilter" aria-label="Filter by grade"><option value="">All grades</option></select>
            <select id="viewMode" aria-label="Which attempts">
              <option value="all">Every attempt</option>
              <option value="latest">Latest per student</option>
              <option value="best">Best per student</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="resultsTable"></table></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="resultsNote"></p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-links" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-links" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Site links</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">These show as icons on the website (portal, register and find-a-student pages) and change as soon as you press Save. Leave a box empty to hide that icon. Web addresses must start with https://</p>
          <form id="linksForm" class="lk-form" novalidate></form>
          <div class="lk-bar">
            <button type="button" class="primary" id="linksSave">Save</button>
            <span class="lk-msg" id="linksMsg" role="status"></span>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-videos" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-videos" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Chapter video tutorials</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">Paste the YouTube address of the video for each chapter. Students see it inside that chapter of the Mentorship Handbook. A chapter with no address shows "coming soon". Unlisted videos work too, but they must allow embedding.</p>
          <form id="videosForm" class="lk-form" novalidate></form>
          <div class="lk-bar">
            <button type="button" class="primary" id="videosSave">Save</button>
            <span class="lk-msg" id="videosMsg" role="status"></span>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-database" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-database" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <div class="adm-card-head">
            <h3>Database</h3>
            <a class="btn ghost" id="shOpen" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" hidden>Open the Google Sheet</a>
          </div>
          <div class="sh-picker" id="shPicker" role="group" aria-label="Choose a table"></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="shHelp"></p>
          <div class="filters">
            <input id="shSearch" type="search" placeholder="Search this table" aria-label="Search this table" />
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="shAdd" hidden>Add</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="shReload">Reload</button>
          </div>
          <div class="tablewrap sh-wrap"><table class="adm-table sh-table" id="shTable"></table></div>
          <div class="sh-pager" id="shPager" hidden>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="shPrev">Previous</button>
            <span id="shPage"></span>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="shNext">Next</button>
          </div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="shNote" role="status"></p>
        </article>
      </div>
    </section>
  </main>

  <!-- One record: add, edit or delete -->
  <dialog id="shDlg" class="adm-drill sh-dlg" aria-labelledby="shDlgTitle">
    <form id="shForm" class="drill-box" novalidate>
      <div class="drill-head">
        <h2 id="shDlgTitle">Row</h2>
        <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="shDlgClose">Close</button>
      </div>
      <p class="adm-note" id="shDlgNote"></p>
      <div class="lk-form sh-fields" id="shFields"></div>
      <p class="formErr" id="shDlgErr" role="alert"></p>
      <div class="lk-bar">
        <button type="submit" class="primary" id="shSave">Save changes</button>
        <button type="button" class="btn ghost sh-del" id="shDelete" hidden>Delete</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </dialog>

  <!-- The list behind whichever statistic was clicked -->
  <dialog id="drill" class="adm-drill" aria-labelledby="drillTitle">
    <div class="drill-box">
      <div class="drill-head">
        <h2 id="drillTitle">Details</h2>
        <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="drillClose">Close</button>
      </div>
      <p class="adm-note drill-summary" id="drillSummary" role="status"></p>
      <div class="filters">
        <input id="drillSearch" type="search" placeholder="Search this list" aria-label="Search this list" />
        <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="drillCsv">Download CSV</button>
      </div>
      <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="drillTable"></table></div>
      <p class="adm-note" id="drillNote"></p>
    </div>
  </dialog>

  <script src="/admin.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

#### `public/admin.js`

```js
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var SS_KEY = "abecfx_admin";
  var MONTH = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
  var NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";

  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  var password = "";
  var stats = null;
  var results = [];
  var scale = [];
  // The Database view: meta = what each table allows, data = the table being looked at, row = the record open in the form.
  var sh = { meta: null, key: "students", data: null, page: 0, mode: "edit", row: null, flash: "" };
  var shSeq = 0;
  var SH_PAGE = 50;

  function getSaved() { try { return sessionStorage.getItem(SS_KEY) || ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }
  function save(v) { try { if (v) sessionStorage.setItem(SS_KEY, v); else sessionStorage.removeItem(SS_KEY); } catch (e) {} }

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }
  function svg(tag, attrs) {
    var e = document.createElementNS(NS, tag);
    Object.keys(attrs || {}).forEach(function (k) { e.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); });
    return e;
  }
  function pct(n, total) { return total ? Math.round((n / total) * 100) : 0; }

  // ---------- API ----------
  function api(path, method, body) {
    var opts = { method: method || "GET", headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + password } };
    if (body) { opts.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"; opts.body = JSON.stringify(body); }
    return fetch(path, opts).then(function (r) {
      return r.json().then(function (b) {
        if (r.status === 401) { var e = new Error(b.error || "Wrong password."); e.auth = true; throw e; }
        if (!r.ok) { var e2 = new Error(b.error || "Something went wrong."); e2.fields = b.errors; e2.status = r.status; throw e2; }
        return b;
      });
    });
  }

  function showLogin(msg) {
    // Leave no student details on the screen once signed out.
    shSeq++;
    sh.meta = null; sh.data = null; sh.row = null; sh.flash = "";
    if ($("shDlg").open) $("shDlg").close();
    $("shTable").replaceChildren();
    $("shSearch").value = "";
    $("dash").hidden = true;
    $("topActions").hidden = true;
    $("login").hidden = false;
    $("loginErr").textContent = msg || "";
  }

  function load() {
    return Promise.all([api("/api/admin/stats"), api("/api/admin/results")]).then(function (res) {
      stats = res[0];
      results = res[1].results;
      scale = res[1].scale;
      save(password);
      $("login").hidden = true;
      $("dash").hidden = false;
      $("topActions").hidden = false;
      $("stamp").textContent = "Updated " + stats.generatedAt;
      renderOverview();
      renderQuiz();
    });
  }

  // ---------- Building blocks ----------
  function bars(container, items, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var total = opts.total != null ? opts.total : items.reduce(function (n, i) { return n + i.count; }, 0);
    var limit = opts.limit || 0;
    container.replaceChildren();
    if (!items.length) { container.appendChild(el("p", "empty", "No data yet.")); return; }
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, items.map(function (i) { return i.count; })) || 1;
    var list = el("div", "bars");
    container.appendChild(list);
    function draw(all) {
      list.replaceChildren();
      (all || !limit ? items : items.slice(0, limit)).forEach(function (i) {
        var row = el(opts.drill ? "button" : "div", "bar");
        if (opts.drill) {
          row.type = "button";
          row.setAttribute("aria-label", i.label + ": " + i.count + ". Show the list");
          row.addEventListener("click", function () { var d = opts.drill(i); openDrill(d.kind, d.value, row); });
        }
        row.appendChild(el("span", "lbl", i.label));
        row.title = i.label;
        var track = el("span", "track");
        var fill = el("span", "fill");
        fill.style.width = (i.count ? Math.max(2, (i.count / max) * 100) : 0) + "%";
        track.appendChild(fill);
        row.appendChild(track);
        var num = el("span", "num", String(i.count));
        num.appendChild(el("small", null, pct(i.count, total) + "%"));
        row.appendChild(num);
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    draw(false);
    if (limit && items.length > limit) {
      var more = el("button", "btn ghost more", "Show all " + items.length);
      more.type = "button";
      var open = false;
      more.addEventListener("click", function () {
        open = !open;
        draw(open);
        more.textContent = open ? "Show fewer" : "Show all " + items.length;
      });
      container.appendChild(more);
    }
  }

  // A headline number. With a drill target it is a button that opens the list behind it.
  function kpi(label, value, target) {
    var d = el(target ? "button" : "div", "kpi");
    if (target) {
      d.type = "button";
      d.setAttribute("aria-label", label + ": " + value + ". Show the list");
      d.addEventListener("click", function () { openDrill(target.kind, target.value, d); });
    }
    d.appendChild(el("b", null, String(value)));
    d.appendChild(el("span", null, label));
    if (target) d.appendChild(el("em", "kpi-cue", "View list \u203a"));
    return d;
  }

  // Make an SVG shape behave like a button (mouse, touch and keyboard).
  function svgButton(node, labelText, target) {
    node.setAttribute("tabindex", "0");
    node.setAttribute("role", "button");
    node.setAttribute("aria-label", labelText + ". Show the list");
    var go = function () { openDrill(target.kind, target.value, node); };
    node.addEventListener("click", go);
    node.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) { if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") { e.preventDefault(); go(); } });
    return node;
  }

  // Column chart; points = [{label, value}]
  function columns(container, points, color) {
    container.replaceChildren();
    var W = 720, H = 220, L = 30, B = 26, T = 14, R = 8;
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, points.map(function (p) { return p.value; }).concat([1]));
    var s = svg("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 " + W + " " + H, role: "img", "aria-label": "Column chart" });
    [0, 0.5, 1].forEach(function (f) {
      var y = T + (H - T - B) * (1 - f);
      s.appendChild(svg("line", { x1: L, x2: W - R, y1: y, y2: y, "class": "grid-line" }));
      var t = svg("text", { x: L - 6, y: y + 4, "text-anchor": "end" });
      t.textContent = Math.round(max * f);
      s.appendChild(t);
    });
    var slot = (W - L - R) / points.length;
    points.forEach(function (p, i) {
      var h = (H - T - B) * (p.value / max);
      var x = L + slot * i + slot * 0.18;
      var r = svg("rect", { x: x, y: H - B - h, width: slot * 0.64, height: h, rx: 4, "class": "bar-rect" });
      s.appendChild(r);
      if (p.drill) {
        var hit = svg("rect", { x: L + slot * i, y: T, width: slot, height: H - T - B, "class": "hit" });
        var tt = svg("title");
        tt.textContent = p.label + ": " + p.value + " (click for the list)";
        hit.appendChild(tt);
        s.appendChild(svgButton(hit, p.label + ": " + p.value, p.drill));
      }
      if (p.value) {
        var v = svg("text", { x: x + slot * 0.32, y: H - B - h - 4, "text-anchor": "middle" });
        v.textContent = p.value;
        s.appendChild(v);
      }
      var lab = svg("text", { x: x + slot * 0.32, y: H - 8, "text-anchor": "middle" });
      lab.textContent = p.label;
      s.appendChild(lab);
    });
    var wrap = el("div", "chart");
    wrap.appendChild(s);
    container.appendChild(wrap);
  }

  // Bars = new per month, line = running total
  function growthChart(container, months) {
    container.replaceChildren();
    if (!months.length) { container.appendChild(el("p", "empty", "No joining dates yet.")); return; }
    var W = 720, H = 260, L = 34, B = 30, T = 16, R = 34;
    var maxNew = Math.max.apply(null, months.map(function (m) { return m.new; }).concat([1]));
    var maxTot = Math.max.apply(null, months.map(function (m) { return m.total; }).concat([1]));
    var s = svg("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 " + W + " " + H, role: "img", "aria-label": "Monthly growth chart" });
    [0, 0.5, 1].forEach(function (f) {
      var y = T + (H - T - B) * (1 - f);
      s.appendChild(svg("line", { x1: L, x2: W - R, y1: y, y2: y, "class": "grid-line" }));
      var a = svg("text", { x: L - 6, y: y + 4, "text-anchor": "end" });
      a.textContent = Math.round(maxNew * f);
      s.appendChild(a);
      var b = svg("text", { x: W - R + 6, y: y + 4 });
      b.textContent = Math.round(maxTot * f);
      s.appendChild(b);
    });
    var slot = (W - L - R) / months.length;
    var step = Math.ceil(months.length / 12);
    var pts = [];
    months.forEach(function (m, i) {
      var h = (H - T - B) * (m.new / maxNew);
      var x = L + slot * i + slot * 0.15;
      var r = svg("rect", { x: x, y: H - B - h, width: slot * 0.7, height: h, rx: 3, "class": "bar-rect" });
      s.appendChild(r);
      var hit = svg("rect", { x: L + slot * i, y: T, width: slot, height: H - T - B, "class": "hit" });
      var tt = svg("title");
      tt.textContent = label(m.ym) + ": " + m.new + " new, " + m.total + " total (click for the students who joined)";
      hit.appendChild(tt);
      s.appendChild(svgButton(hit, label(m.ym) + ": " + m.new + " new, " + m.total + " total", { kind: "month", value: m.ym }));
      pts.push([x + slot * 0.35, T + (H - T - B) * (1 - m.total / maxTot)]);
      if (i % step === 0) {
        var t = svg("text", { x: x + slot * 0.35, y: H - 10, "text-anchor": "middle" });
        t.textContent = label(m.ym);
        s.appendChild(t);
      }
    });
    s.appendChild(svg("polyline", { points: pts.map(function (p) { return p.join(","); }).join(" "), "class": "line" }));
    pts.forEach(function (p, i) {
      var dot = svg("circle", { cx: p[0], cy: p[1], r: 6, "class": "dot" });
      var dt = svg("title");
      dt.textContent = label(months[i].ym) + ": " + months[i].total + " students in total (click for the list)";
      dot.appendChild(dt);
      s.appendChild(svgButton(dot, label(months[i].ym) + ": " + months[i].total + " students in total", { kind: "upto", value: months[i].ym }));
    });
    var wrap = el("div", "chart");
    wrap.appendChild(s);
    container.appendChild(wrap);
  }

  function label(ym) {
    var p = ym.split("-");
    return MONTH[Number(p[1]) - 1] + " " + p[0].slice(2);
  }

  function table(tbl, head, rows) {
    tbl.replaceChildren();
    var thead = el("thead"), tr = el("tr");
    head.forEach(function (h) { tr.appendChild(el("th", h.n ? "n" : "", h.t)); });
    thead.appendChild(tr);
    tbl.appendChild(thead);
    var tb = el("tbody");
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var row = el("tr");
      r.forEach(function (c, i) {
        var td = el("td", head[i].n ? "n" : "");
        if (c && c.node) td.appendChild(c.node); else td.textContent = c == null ? "" : String(c);
        row.appendChild(td);
      });
      tb.appendChild(row);
    });
    tbl.appendChild(tb);
  }

  // A table cell that opens a list.
  function linkCell(text, kind, value) {
    var b = el("button", "linkbtn", String(text));
    b.type = "button";
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { openDrill(kind, value, b); });
    return { node: b };
  }

  function gradeBadge(g) { return { node: el("span", "grade g-" + g, g) }; }

  // ---------- Overview ----------
  function renderOverview() {
    var s = stats;
    var k = $("kpis");
    k.replaceChildren(
      kpi("Total students", s.total, { kind: "all" }),
      kpi("Certified / graduates", s.graduates, { kind: "graduates" }),
      kpi("Uncertified", s.uncertified, { kind: "uncertified" }),
      kpi("Mentors, staff and CEO", s.team, { kind: "team" }),
      kpi("Took the quiz", s.quiz.students, { kind: "quiz-students" }),
      kpi("Average quiz score", s.quiz.avgPct == null ? "-" : s.quiz.avgPct + "%", { kind: "quiz-avg" })
    );
    var by = function (kind) { return function (i) { return { kind: kind, value: i.label }; }; };
    bars($("c-status"), s.status.filter(function (x) { return x.count > 0; }), { total: s.total, drill: by("status") });
    $("c-status-note").textContent = "Certified / graduates = Certified, Graduate and Alumni Member.";
    bars($("c-gender"), s.gender, { drill: by("gender") });
    bars($("c-programme"), s.programme, { drill: by("programme") });
    bars($("c-year"), s.byYear, { drill: by("year") });
    bars($("c-country"), s.country, { limit: 10, drill: by("country") });
```
